months of the year), (2) new Kunshan capacity (total 1.2mn sheets/month or 21% of the
company’s current capacity) ramp up from December, and (3) the mass volume
shipment for several new AI projects (Vera Rubin + TPU v8 etc.) with a better demand
volume on the most high end M8.5/M9 CCL, we now expect EMC’s revenue to go up by
20% QoQ, and expect the GM will further expand to 40.9% (first time surpassing
40%).
For TUC, with an even faster product mix improvement speed (due to the lower base vs.

EMC) and the solid pricing hike uptrend for both high end and mid end CCL (15-20%
QoQ average pricing hike in 4Q), we expect the company to deliver a revenue growth
rate at 18% QoQ, and believe the GM will reach 40.8% (+3.9ppt QoQ) in 4Q26,
despite the slowing demand from key AI ASIC customer due to the transition period of
the ASIC projects whose capacity will be replaced by other high-end Ethernet switch
projects (also M8.5/M9 grade CCL).
Key update - EMC now plan to expand capacity to 15.25mn sheets/month from

5.85mn sheets/month capacity today, with solid demand
EMC’s BOD has approved additional capacity expansion plans extending into
2029/30E. EMC now targets year-end capacity of 8.70/10.45/11.95/15.25mn
sheets/month in 2027/28/29/30E (implying 27% 2026-30E CAGR), which is the first
time EMC announced its new capacity expansion plan after 1H28. EMC mentioned that
capacity expansion is supported by LTAs with customers who secure volumes (EMC does
not proactively initiate LTA negotiations). EMC indicated that despite its proactive

capacity expansion planned for 2027-30E, it would still be insufficient to meet with
customer demand secured under signed LTAs (demand under LTA extends at least 3Y
ahead).
EMC’s near-term capacity expansion remains intact, as the capacity expansion in
Kunshan (with additional 1.2mn sheets/month, 21% of current capacity) could
come online as early as Dec 2026 and continue through May 2027 (with new capacity
coming online every month), the expansion was originally scheduled in 2 phases, with

600k sheets/month each coming online in 1Q27 and 3Q27. The accelerated ramp-up
will offset the rescheduling of Guanyin capacity expansion, where the 300k sheet/month
expansions originally scheduled for 4Q26 have been deferred to 2H27, mainly due to
shortages of construction capacity in Taiwan as well as long equipment lead time +
Taiwan government’s strict policy on labor safety related requirement (however, we only
see delay in Taiwan, while all other plans are basically in-line or earlier than the

company’s original expectation).
Investment view - Solid margin beat + expanding margin, suggesting a stock
performance outlook; buy in the dip for both EMC & TUC
We notice that the BBG consensus for 2027 EMC/TUC GM is only at 38%/34%, and we
see upside risk to consensus EPS estimates for both EMC/TUC as the GM in 4Q could
both surpass 40-42%, without any possibility to see the GM to decrease in coming
quarters as the high-end CCL supply demand gap to further expand in coming years (see

here). As a result, we believe consensus 2027 GM could lift from 34-38% to 40-42%+ for
both companies in coming days, suggesting 10-25% EPS upward revision opportunity,
and recommend taking advantage to buy in this valuation dip (EMC/TUC only trades at
15x/12x of our bullish 2027 EPS estimate, which was far below the past 3 year range of

20-30x).  
Moreover, EMC has mentioned all of its new capacity in coming years (capacity will 
expand by 2.6x in 2030 vs. 2026; see more details above) is still not large enough to 
support customers’ LTAs volume demand, suggesting an even more favorable high-end 
CCL pricing outlook in coming years (all the way to 2030), which is in-line with what we 
discussed in our TPCA takeaway report. We expect EMC/TUC will rise the like for like 
base pricing for M7+ CCL by 10-40%, and we now expect the similar pricing hike ratio in 
2027/28, with more room to adjust if the supply shortage issue continues.  
For the new CCL generation, we exect EMC to start shipment on M10 grade CCL from 
2H27, which is only for single customers’ AI GPU projects, but still implies a CCL 
migration trend in-line with our bullish view. For M9, we only see HGX R200 UBB and 
VR200 LPU adopt this solution in 2026, but see multiple new projects in 2027 will adopt 
this high ASP project (including several 1.6T ethernet switch projects, and Trainium 4/ 
TPU v9 could also see at least 50% adoption rate of the M9 CCL; moreover, the VR300 
rack should have 50%+ of its CCL content related to M9 grade CCL). We believe this new 
technology migration trend going into 2027 will further drive both companies’ ASP and 
profit in coming quarters, suggesting an even more favorable outlook for EMC/TUC’s EPS 
growth in coming years.  
Overall, we maintain our bullish view on high-end CCL players’ business outlook and 
margin expansion trend, and expect investors to start focusing on this solid margin 
expansion trend after this clean beat on GM/OPM in 3Q26; maintain Buy on 
EMC/TUC, with a new TP of NT$11,700/NT$3,460 (from NT$10,200/NT$3,010).

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Elite Material  
EMC, a key supplier of high-end HDI material (70%+ market share) and SLP material 
(90%+ market share), has been focusing on the high-speed switch/server CCL market for

### Page 7

3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and 
(3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$11,700 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising 
trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3) 
rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$3,460 is based on a 22x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(+2x STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

방위산업

◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 1,640,000원 유지 
◼ 컨센서스 및 추정치 대비 매출, 영업이익 하회 
◼ 4Q26 및 27년 실적 추정치 상향

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 1,640,000원 유지 
4Q26E-2Q27E 지상방산 및 항공우주 EBITDA에 Target 12MF EV/EBITDA 25.0x 
적용, 상장 자회사 가치 SOTP 통해 목표주가 산출. 3Q26 실적 아쉬우나 투자포
인트에는 변함 없어 멀티플은 변경 없이 적용. 27년 실적 추정치 상향에 따라 본
업 가치 상승, 26년 스페인 자주포 양산 및 미국 자주포 시제 사업자로 선정되며 
현지화 전략이 유효했음을 증명. 3Q26 실적에 대한 우려를 소화하며 글로벌 피어
들과의 멀티플 격차 축소를 전망함

컨센서스 및 추정치 대비 매출, 영업이익 하회 
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 6.1조원(-6.0% YoY), 영업이익 4,520억원(-46.2% YoY, 
OPM 7.4%)으로 컨센서스인 매출액 7.4조원 및 영업이익 1.1조원을 크게 하회
하는 실적 시현. 하회의 주요 요인으로는 3Q26 필리조선소 적자폭 확대, 지상
방산 부문 실적이 부진한 영향. 지상방산 부문의 경우 매출액 1.2조원(-41.0% 
YoY), 영업이익 220억원(-96.2% YoY, OPM 1.7%)을 시현했는데, 폴란드 관련 
매출이 부재했던 1Q26 영업이익률 16.9%보다도 낮은 실적을 보임. 이는 국내
외 사업 모두에서 악재가 겹쳐 발생했던 것으로 보이는데 국내 사업에서는 마
진율이 낮은 사업이 매출로 인식되었고 수출 사업에서는 폴란드 매출 부재 뿐 
아니라 이집트, 호주 K9 자주포 매출 인식 또한 예상 대비 부진해 고정비 레버
리지 효과로 3Q26 영업이익률이 크게 훼손된 것으로 추정. 이외 대전사업장 복
구에 따른 비용, 기타 사업 충당금 등 총 500억원 수준의 일회성 비용이 발생했
으며 영업외손익에서는 원화 강세에 따른 환손실이 주로 반영되었음

4Q26 및 27년 실적 추정치 상향 
1Q26에서 3Q26까지 이뤄지지 않았던 폴란드 EC2 K9 자주포 30문 인도는 
4Q26에 집중될 것으로 보이며 폴란드 EC1 천무 또한 기존 가이던스였던 26년 
천무 40대에서 60대로 인도 대수 상향이 이뤄짐. 이에 따라 4Q26 지상방산 영
업이익률은 22.8%로 회복을 전망. 27년에는 폴란드 K9 자주포 및 천무 인도 대
수 증가로 26년 수출 비중 58.0%에서 77.2%로 증가, 이에 따라 영업이익률 또
한 매출 믹스 개선에 따라 19.8%에서 28.0%로 개선을 전망

Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -32.53 điểm tương ứng -1.83%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Linh kiện ô tô, Dầu khí và Công nghệ là các nhóm ngành có m ức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -634 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VPL; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-609 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -5,829 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -1,462 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 722 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -5,278 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam đánh mất đà hồi phục của tuần trước, khi VN-Index quay đầu giảm khoảng 
1,8% về đóng cửa quanh 1.742 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm lùi sâu về sát 1.727 
điểm trước khi thu hẹp đà giảm. Dù mùa báo cáo KQKD quý 3 diễn ra tương đối tích cực, 
tuy nhiên các lo ngại về việc môi trường lãi suất cao vẫn duy trì trong bối cảnh căng thẳng 
Trung Đông leo thang đang đè nặng lên tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Kết quả kỳ họp của FED trong 
tuần qua nhìn chung phù hợp với kỳ vọng và không gây ra ảnh hưởng đáng kể. Điểm kém 
tích cực nhất là khối ngoại đã quay lại bán ròng với tổng giá trị khoảng 2.700 tỷ đồng 
trong tuần — đảo ngược diễn biến mua ròng cuối tuần trước, cho thấy đà rút vốn ngoại 
vẫn chưa thực sự dừng lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến giảm trên đồ thị tuần, nhưng chưa thoái lui quá 1 
nửa cây nến tăng của tuần trước, cùng thanh khoản được tiết giảm, hàm ý nhịp điều chỉnh 
vẫn trong phạm vi cho phép. Trên khung ngày, sau bốn phiên giằng co đầu tuần với thanh 
khoản thấp, chỉ số đã chịu áp lực sụt giảm mạnh hơn trong phiên cuối tuần, chớm xuống 
dưới đường tín hiệu chính giữa dải Bollinger, nhưng cho tín hiệu rút chân ngay sau đó, thể 
hiện áp lực bán tháo đã tạm thời được kiểm soát. Mặc dù rủi ro mở rộng thêm nhịp điều 
chỉnh vẫn còn để ngỏ, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn đang được bảo lưu, VNINDEX 
được kỳ vọng sẽ cho phản ứng hồi phục trở lại tại các điểm đỡ bên dưới.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1850 (+-15)

Hỗ trợ gần: 1720 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1680 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

## Document 362: European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30)

Source PDF: all report pdf/European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

European resilience, American vigour

Eurozone growth continues to prove resilient, notwithstanding the volatility of the Irish figures. The Q3 data reinforce 
our forecast of 1.1% growth this year, which could even be slightly exceeded, as German and Spanish momentum shows 
no sign of fading. In France, the negative surprise (-0.1% q/q against a consensus of +0.1% q/q) stems from a new one-
off factor (the sharp fall in aerospace output caused by delays in input deliveries) and masks an encouraging rebound 
in household consumption. In the United  States, growth reached its highest level since Q2 2025 (0.8% q/q), driven by 
buoyant private domestic demand.

Eurozone: The resilience of growth is confirmed 
Eurozone GDP grew by 0.4% q/q in Q3  according to Eurostat's preliminary flash estimate, above both the consensus and our 
Nowcast (0.3%). The ever -volatile Irish figures contributed 0.2 pp to this outcome. Excluding Ireland, growth (0.2% q/q) 
nonetheless remains in line with the pace of recent quarters (0.2 -0.3% q/q on average). This illustrates the Eurozone's capacity 
to withstand the current shocks, thanks in particular to the strength of Spain (+0.07 pp contribution to the q/q change in Eurozone’s 
GDP) and the Netherlands (+0.04 pp), and to Germany's gradual recovery (+0.06 pp). France (-0.03 pp) and Italy (zero growth), 
by contrast, posted disappointing results, although in France's case these partly reflect temporary factors (aerospace output). The 
growth carry-over for 2026 now stands at 1.0%, which supports our forecast of 1.1% in annual average terms and even points to 
upside risk. The European Commission's October surveys remain upbeat, with a further improvement in industrial activity 
expectations. 
The German recovery continues . As anticipated by our  Nowcast, real GDP grew by 0.2% q/q in Q3 (after 0.3% in Q2).  
According to Destatis, consumption (private and public) increased slightly, while investment edged down. Export momentum 
slowed markedly compared with Q2. This is Germany's fourth consecutive quarter of growth. 
Italian GDP surprised on the downside, stagnating in Q3 (against a consensus of 0.1%).  According to Istat, this reflects a 
decline in value added in industry, offset by increases in services and agriculture. The positive contribution from domestic demand 
was reportedly cancelled out by a negative contribution from net trade. 
Spanish growth remained robust in Q3, in line with expectations (0.6% q/q, after 0.7% in Q2),  driven mainly by domestic 
demand (0.5 pp contribution) and, to a lesser extent, by net exports (0.1 pp). Consumption remains the main engine of growth 
(+0.6% q/q for private consumption and +0.9% for public consumption). Investment rose by 0.7%. Export gr owth (1.3%) once 
again outpaced import growth (1.1%) this quarter. All sectors except construction recorded positive changes in value added, with 
industry and services both up 0.7%. 
France: A sharp drop in aerospace output prevents a return to growth in Q3 
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q in Q3 (after stagnating in Q2), weighed down by a sharp fall in aerospace output (-4.5% q/q 
in the production of other transport equipment, which includes aerospace). This steep decline dragged down goods production 
(down 1.2% q/q) and explains the sharp contraction in exports ( -1.7% q/q) and business investment (-0.4% q/q). Net trade thus 
subtracted 0.6 pp from GDP (imports were flat over the same period). Conversely, household consumption surprised on the 
upside (+0.4% q/q), boosted by purchases of electric vehicles (registrations up 112% y/y in Q3). Public consumption 
(+0.3% q/q) also supported growth, as did activity in market services (+0.3% q/q).  Despite the sharp drop in aerospace 
output in Q3, Airbus has confirmed its delivery targets for the year (with engine supply problems expected to be resolved), pointing 
to a strong rebound in growth in Q4 (0.3% q/q according to our forecasts). Overall, gr owth is expected to reach 0.3% in 2026 , 
before picking up to 0.8% in 2027. 
United States: Growth at a one-year high  
GDP growth picked up in Q3 to 3.4% at a seasonally adjusted annualised rate ( AR; i.e. 0.8% q/q), from 2.2% in Q2 
(0.6% q/q). This is the fastest pace since Q3 2025, confirming that activity is almost immune to the energy shock. Private domestic 
demand remains particularly strong. Private non-residential investment accelerated to 12.7% AR, a performance unmatched since 
the post-Covid reopening. AI-related components are driving this figure (18.5% AR), but other types of investment are also rising 
for a second consecutive quarter (3.8% AR) after contracting uninterruptedly throughout 2025. Household consumption, steady 
at 2.5% AR in real terms, is growing faster than it did before inflation picked up again, but at the cost of a significant drop in the 
saving rate (3.8% at the end of the quarter, down from 4.4% at the end of the previous quarter). The United States nonetheles s 
continues to "import its growth": net trade is weighing on GDP owing to a surge in imports (19.7% AR), driven by AI -related 
components – IT, semiconductors and telecommunications (103.8% AR).

### Page 2

Stéphane Colliac 
Head of Advanced Economies Team, France  
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

Lucie Barette 
Economist, Germany, Italy and Spain 
lucie.barette@bnpparibas.com

Economist, United States, Japan

## Document 375: FX Daily_ Democratic sweep can weigh on the dollar

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ Democratic sweep can weigh on the dollar.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

We don’t expect today’s US midterm results to cause structural
shifts in FX, but the dollar may face some short-term pressure
should the Democrats secure both the House (widely expected)
and the Senate (close call). Elsewhere, a dovish hike by the
RBA has hit AUD, but we remain optimistic on the outlook for
the currency beyond the near term

Francesco Pesole, Frantisek Taborsky and Chris Turner
We still expect the FX impact from the midterms to be short-lived
USD: Some downside risk if Democrats
win it all
All 435 House of Representatives seats and 35 of the 100 Senate
seats are up for election today in the US. The Democrats are widely
expected to comfortably take the House, where they need a net
gain of just four seats. The Senate is expected to be much closer,
though prediction markets still give the Democrats a 74% chance of
securing it.

### Page 2

We discussed three election scenarios in this note a couple of weeks
ago. Our baseline assumption is that markets fully price in a
Republican loss of the House, but only partially a Democratic clean
sweep. A blue sweep can put some pressure on the dollar. Fears of
tougher regulation could weigh on AI stocks (we’ve recently seen
how that can hit the greenback), while markets could price in
greater risks of decision-making gridlock around issues such as

government funding and the debt ceiling.
Still, we doubt that’s enough to trigger a structural directional shift
in the dollar. The greenback continues to respond predominantly to
the Fed story, which shouldn’t face an immediate and direct impact
from the midterm result. We expect a split Congress (R-S, D-H) to
have a neutral or slightly positive USD impact.
On timing, polls close between midnight and 3.00 AM GMT. US
media outlets may have already called the House for the

Democrats before the London open tomorrow. The tighter Senate
races may take much longer to call, possibly days, partly because
of mail-in ballot counting delays and potential legal disputes. A
close eye will be on seven toss-up Senate races: Alaska, Iowa,
Kansas, Maine, Michigan, Nebraska, Ohio and Texas. In six of those,
the seat is currently held by a Republican.
Elsewhere, oil prices have been the key drivers in FX at the start of
the week. Hopes of a revamp in diplomatic eﬀorts have sent Brent

tumbling back below $105/bl, while the dollar has softened slightly
alongside commodity currencies. On the macro side, today’s
calendar includes US JOLTS job openings.
Francesco Pesole
EUR: ECB speakers have stayed hawkish
EUR/USD is looking to establish support at 1.120 after a good start
to the week. A Democratic sweep at the midterms today could help,
but we don’t expect radical mood changes in the pair.
French spreads didn’t beneﬁt from lower oil prices yesterday,

signalling levels above 150bp are the new normal now. It therefore
remains hard for the euro to shake out the lingering risk premium,

### Page 3

and much more tangible progress is needed for oil to trade

In the eurozone, focus will be on a number of ECB speakers today.
Since last week’s meeting, we’ve heard some hawkish comments,
endorsing market expectations that at least another hike is needed.
Elevated energy prices suggest there is little room for a clear dovish
current to emerge yet.
Francesco Pesole
AUD: Dovish hike doesn't hit Aussie
dollar's medium term outlook

The Aussie dollar came under pressure this morning after the RBA
hiked rates as expected to 4.85%, but delivered some dovish
signals. First, the 6-3 split suggests lower conviction around today’s
decision than in markets, which had almost fully priced in a hike
(21bp). Second, the statement dropped its reference to potential
further hikes for the ﬁrst time since June. Third, the economic
outlook was revised sharply to the downside, with a focus on falling

house prices weighing on consumers and upside risks to
unemployment.
At the press conference, Governor Michele Bullock reinforced the
RBA’s wait-and-see approach, stressing that monetary tightening
still has to feed through to the economy. She left the door open to
further tightening, but the bar is clearly higher now. The RBA
appears happy to assess the impact of previous hikes before acting
again.
This is in line with our forecast of no further RBA hikes. Despite

today’s correction, we remain positive on AUD, which oﬀers the
best short-term carry in G10, including on a volatility-adjusted
basis. Markets price only a one-in-three chance of another hike at
the coming meetings and already embed 13bp of easing by the
end of 2027, leaving AUD with limited risk of further dovish repricing
for now. The current environment remains a bit challenging for AUD
amid AI-related jitters, but we remain conﬁdent that our 0.710

AUD/USD target can be reached by year-end.

### Page 4

Francesco Pesole
CEE: Zloty is the weakest link in the
region
Yesterday’s PMI readings in the region conﬁrmed the economy's
strong position across the board. The outlook remains positive.
With the exception of Turkey, all countries show PMI levels higher
than those seen before the onset of the US-Iran conﬂict. The Czech
Republic and Hungary appear particularly strong, hovering near or
reaching new highs; Poland is edging higher, while Romania has

declined once again. Overall, however, the economy continues to
show resilience, pointing to a stable outlook for the coming
months.
Today, Turkey’s inﬂation data for October is released. We expect
monthly inﬂation to accelerate from 1.8% to 2.4%, driven by
increased energy cost pressures, which the Central Bank of Turkey
has identiﬁed as an upside risk, as well as seasonal rises in food
and clothing prices. This would result in a slight decline in annual

inﬂation, from 29.7% in September to 29.6%. This should be
suﬃcient to allow for at least one more rate cut by the CBT this
year.
EUR/PLN tested new highs yesterday following political headlines
over the weekend. In our view, these developments provide further
bearish momentum, suggesting additional downside for the zloty.
Naturally, global conditions remain unfavourable, characterised by
a strong US dollar and high energy prices, despite some
improvement yesterday. Furthermore, Poland’s public ﬁnance

deﬁcit, the highest in the EU, leaves the country exposed to
potential turmoil in the euro-denominated bond market.
Additionally, we anticipate a dovish tone from the National Bank of
Poland at this week’s meeting. Taken together, we continue to view
the zloty as the weakest link in the CEE region and expect the
EUR/PLN pair to settle above 4.420.
Frantisek Taborsky

## Document 432: Sukuk_ Structure, stability and strategic opportunity (for Private Bank only)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Sukuk_ Structure, stability and strategic opportunity (for Private Bank only).pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Thematic Reports)
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 15
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

WS Global CIO Office

InvesTips

Sukuk: Structure,  
stability and strategic 
opportunity

September 2026

### Page 2

InvesTips: Sukuk: Structure, stability and strategic opportunity  |  September 2026  2

Steve Brice 
Global Chief Investment Officer

Manpreet Gill 
Chief Investment Officer, EMEA

Hannah Chew 
Portfolio Strategist

Islamic finance is growing at a rapid pace, driven 
primarily by increasing demand for investors who 
seek Shariah -compliant investments. Sukuk, often 
referred to as Islamic bonds, surpassed USD 1trn in 
2025, making them one of the fastest -growing 
segments o f Islamic finance. The asset class’s 
unique value proposition is driving its popularity, 
especially in an increasingly volatile global  
economic landscape. 
In this publication, we will explore the evolving role of Sukuk in wealth management, 
examining market fundamentals, risk frameworks and emerging opportunities within 
this market.

### Page 3

InvesTips: Sukuk: Structure, stability and strategic opportunity  |  September 2026  3

Sukuk, commonly referred to as Islamic bonds, are Shariah-compliant investment 
certificates representing ownership interests in underlying assets, usufructs (the 
right to use an asset and receive its benefits, without owning it) or ventures. In 
return, Sukuk holders receive periodic distributions, typically in the form of rental 
payments (Ijarah), profit-sharing (Mudarabah) or asset-generated cash flows, and 
upon maturity the principal amount is repaid. Sukuk differ from conventional 
bonds in several ways: 
Fig. 1  
Sukuk vs. conventional bonds

Aspect  Conventional bonds Sukuk

Core principle  Lending (time-value of money) Asset ownership 
Underlying asset  Any asset, business or project Must be Sharia-compliant 
Returns  Guaranteed (fixed or floating interest) Not legally guaranteed (depends on 
underlying asset’s performance) 
Risk   Issuer bears all performance risk Shared risk between parties 
Transparency  Moderate (depends on disclosure) High (asset identification required)

Source: Standard Chartered

The role of Sukuk in the investing landscape is evolving amid 
global uncertainty and rising geopolitical tensions. Investors 
are increasingly seeking more stable and asset ‑backed 
alternatives. An investor can consider investing in Sukuk for 
the following reasons: 
Resilient 
Sukuk provide asset -backed collateral that conventional 
bonds often lack, offering a cushion during volatile market 
conditions. Historically, Islamic investments, including Sukuk’, 
have been countercyclical, i.e. they tend to expand or remain 
stable when c onventional markets contract. Across major 
market stress from the 2018 US-China Trade War through the 
2026 Middle East Conflict, Sukuk bonds consistently 
outperformed Global Bonds — delivering smaller drawdowns 
in risk-off episodes such as the 2018 US-China trade war and 
2022 higher for longer inflation, and greater resilience in 
geopolitical oil shocks, underscoring the asset class's 
structural defensive characteristics.

1 The evolving role of Sukuk

Strong fundamentals
The Sukuk market experienced an unprecedented 25% growth in issuance in 2025, with issuance nearly USD 300bn. According
to some  reports,  the  Sukuk  market  could  reach  USD  3trn  by  2030  driven  by  increased  demand  for  Shariah-compliant
investments, sovereign funds’ diversification needs, development of regulatory frameworks for Sukuk and growth in Middle East

capital markets.  Nearly  80%  of  Fitch ‑rated Sukuk  carry  investment ‑grade ratings  and  have  little  or  no  history  of  default,
underscoring the sector’s strong fundamentals. These factors should help to instil confidence among investors, who can look at
Sukuk as central building blocks of their portfolio.
Fig. 3
Sukuk market size in USD bn
Source: The Business Research Company, Standard Chartered

### Page 5

InvesTips: Sukuk: Structure, stability and strategic opportunity  |  September 2026 5
Convergence with sustainable investing trends
Sukuk and sustainable bonds share more common ground than their different origins suggest, making sustainable Sukuk a
natural bridge between Islamic finance and sustainable investing. This also helps explain Sukuk’s growing appeal beyond
traditional Muslim investors, as evidenced by Sustainable Sukuk issuance rising from USD 464mn in 2017 to USD 9bn in 2024,

representing a CAGR of 52.7%. The overlap starts with what Sukuk exclude: Shariah principles prohibit interest, excessive
uncertainty and speculation, and screen out sectors financing harmful industries such as tobacco, gambling, weapons, adult
entertainment and alcohol – a negative screen that closely aligns with the exclusionary (negative screening) method, commonly
used in sustainable investing. Tradi tional Sukuk bonds require proceeds to be linked to identifiable real assets or projects,

creating a ring-fenced structure like use-of-proceeds sustainable bonds. Combined with Shariah’s broader objectives of social
welfare, resource stewardship and equita ble risk-sharing, this creates strong alignment with the United Nations Sustainable
Development Goals.
Green Sukuk is an established example, where proceeds are earmarked for environmental projects such as renewable energy,
clean transport, energy-efficient buildings or sustainable water and wastewater management. Issuers must meet both Shariah

approval from a recognised scholarly board and alignment with International Capital Market Association (ICMA) Green Bond
Principles.
Traditional Sukuk already comply with Shariah laws such as avoiding Riba (interest -based returns arising from lending
transactions), Gharar (investments with ambiguity) and Maysir (speculation). Sustainable Sukuk build on this foundation by
adding environmental and social objectives. The table below highlights how sustainable Sukuk differ from traditional Sukuk.

Digital integration
The integration  of  blockchain  technology  and  digital  platforms  is  revolutionising  Sukuk  issuance  and  trading,  dramatically
reducing transaction costs and expanding accessibility to  a broader  investor base.  For instance, SAKKEX (an  independent
blockchain-based Sukuk marketplace) streamlines drafting, issuance, and near ‑instant settlement. Its Sukuk Wallet App also
allows investors to trade on the go. Several such digital platforms now allow tokenised Sukuk that enable fractional ownership,

automated Shariah  compliance  verification  and  real-time  settlement,  thereby  addressing  challenges  that  have  persisted  in
Islamic finance markets. Digital tools also simplify cross border Sukuk transactions by enabling end to end digital origination and
trading, with smart contracts automating profit sharing to cut operational costs and enhance transparency.

### Page 6

InvesTips: Sukuk: Structure, stability and strategic opportunity  |  September 2026 6
Sukuk structures define the nature of ownership, risk -sharing and return
generation. Several types of Sukuk structures exist and are defined by the
underlying Shariah contract. We discuss some prominent ones below and see
how they align with various phases of an investor’s wealth journey:
Sukuk al-Ijarah is a Shariah-compliant lease-based bond where the investor gains partial ownership in a physical

asset such as a building, airport or equipment. The asset is then leased back to the issuer who pays a nominal
(pre-agreed) rent to the investor. An investor seeking stable, predictable income streams with lower volatility can
consider investing in these Sukuk. The original principal amount is returned at the end of the lease period.
Ijarah Sukuk are ideal for the wealth preservation phase of an investor’s wealth journey. For instance, investors
nearing retirement typically need to restructure their portfolios by shifting from growth to preservation while

generating steady income to cover living expenses. Ijarah Sukuk deliver on both fronts: protecting capital through
asset-backed structures while providing predictable rental income that supports household needs without
depleting principal.
Sukuk al-Murabahah are cost-plus financing structures where the issuer buys an asset and immediately resells
it to the investors at a marked-up price, with payment deferred over a pre-determined time. Consider this: Let us

assume, a company needs USD 100m. A special purpose vehicle (SPV), established by the company, mobilises
funds from investors and buys commodities worth USD 100m and immediately resells them to the company for
USD 112m payable over time. The USD 12m difference is the agreed profit margin. Murabahah Sukuk ar e best
suited for investors seeking income over a short to medium term, typically, 1-5 years. Investments in this type of
Sukuk return the principal gradually throughout the investment period rather than only at maturity.

Investments in Murabahah Sukuk are best suited for wealth protection and income generation. Unlike Ijarah which
return principal as a lump sum at maturity, Murabahah amortise both profit and principal in each instalment. This
makes them ideal for retirees who need to systematically draw down capital to fund living expenses without
depleting their portfolio too quickly.
Sukuk al-Mudarabah is a profit-sharing partnership arrangement where investors provide 100% of the capital

and operate as silent partners. The issuer manages the business or project as the active partner. Under a
Mudarabah arrangement, financial losses arising from normal bu siness risks are borne by the investors in
proportion to their capital contribution, while the Mudarib loses its effort and entitlement to profit. However, where
losses result from negligence, misconduct, breach of agreed terms, or misconduct by the Mudari b, the Mudarib
may be held liable. Think of it as venture capital meets bonds; you fund the business, professionals run it and

you share the profits or bear the losses resulting from the business activities. Mudarabah Sukuk are often
regarded as one of the  structures most closely aligned with the Shariah principle of profit and loss sharing.
However, they bear higher risk and lower predictability compared to other structures.
Investors with a growth mindset and open to high -risk tolerance can consider investing in these Sukuk . They
best align with accumulation and growth phase of the wealth journey, typically, when investors have long time

horizons to absorb volatility and can afford capital risk for higher returns. However, to balance risk, it is important
for investors to diversify across various types of Sukuk. Even within Mudarabah, they can consider investing
in multiple projects, sectors and geographies.
1 Sukuk structures and
wealth mandates

### Page 7

InvesTips: Sukuk: Structure, stability and strategic opportunity  |  September 2026  7

A joint venture partnership structure where both investors and the issuer contribute capital is Sukuk al -
Musharakah. In this structure, both parties (issuer and investors) jointly provide capital, participate in managing 
the venture, and share profits or losses based on agreed upon ratios. This structure appeals to investors focused 
on growth, interested in active participation, and prepared to take on greater risk in pursuit of higher returns. 
Musharakah Sukuk are best suited for the growth and accumulation phase where investors are building wealth 
aggressively, comfortable with high risk and able to lock up capital for the long term (typically 5+ years).

Sukuk al-Wakalah is an agency arrangement where investors appoint a representative from the issuing company 
as their Wakil (agent) to invest their funds in specific Shariah-compliant assets or projects in exchange for a fee. 
This arrangement is similar to a mutual fund or ETF, where the agent manages a diversified pool of Shariah 
compliant assets on behalf of the investors. Investors benefit from professional fund management, diversification, 
transparency and flexibility. Depending on issuer preferences, Wakalah Sukuk can b e structured with lower 
investment thresholds, making them accessible to a wide range of investors. 
Wakalah Sukuk suitability depends heavily on the underlying asset allocation and risk profile. Conservative 
Wakalah structures investing in stable, income -generating assets suit wealth preservation. Moderate -risk 
Wakalah can complement accumulation portfolios as a diversification tool, though not as a primary growth driver 
as their structure and risk mandate limit upside potential.

Sukuk al-Istisna is a project financing structure based on a manufacturing or construction contract. Investors 
provide upfront capital to finance the construction of specific assets. Upon completion, the asset is sold to an end 
buyer (often the original issuer or a third party) who makes deferred payments including the cost plus a mark-up, 
which are then distributed to investors. Istisna suit seasoned investors with construction finance expertise and 
those who can absorb development risk and commit capital for the long term. Owing to its complexity and higher 
risk profile, this type of Sukuk typically requires large initial investments, making it less accessible to retail 
investors.  
In the wealth cycle, Istisna are best aligned with the accumulation and growth phase, when long time horizons 
can absorb construction delays and development risks. The long-term commitment and potential for cost overruns 
or project failure make it unsuitable for later wealth stages such as retirement.

Sukuk al -Salam is an Islamic bond backed by a forward purchase contract where investors pay upfront for 
commodities to be delivered later. At its core, they are similar to forward contracts. However, unlike a forward 
contract, Salam contracts are fully prepaid, standardi sed and designed with clearly defined delivery terms to 
reduce uncertainty (gharar). Under this arrangement, an investor profits from the difference between the lower 
Salam purchase price (paid today) and the higher resale price (received at delivery).  
Salam Sukuk carry high delivery and price risk and aligns well with investors in the growth and accumulation 
phase. Salam requires strong commodity‑market expertise, deep understanding of production and delivery risks, 
and the ability to assess the issuer’s operational capacity to deliver the specified goods on time.

InvesTips: Sukuk: Structure, stability and strategic opportunity  |  September 2026  8

Owing to their ethical framework and asset -backed nature, Sukuk are often
considered relatively less risky than conventional bonds. However, they are not
immune to risks. Unique challenges such as asset quality deterioration and
structure-specific exposures require rigorous risk management.

Due diligence in Sukuk extends beyond traditional credit
analysis and an investor should also consider the following:
• Shariah compliance verification can be done by evaluating

the Sukuk structure and confirming that its mandates and
underlying assets fully align with Islamic principles.
• Evaluating asset quality by verifying tangibility, cash -
generating capability and legal ownership can help
investor reduce risk.
• Assessing creditworthiness and market depth will help in
identifying financially strong Sukuk issuers and ensuring
adequate liquidity.
• Cross-border Sukuk investments remain viable,
contingent on careful scrutiny of legal documentation

across multiple jurisdictions to ensure enforceability and
investor protection.
Robust due diligence and proactive risk mitigation strategies
are essential to protect capital and stay aligned with Islamic
financial principles.
1 Risk management and due diligence

### Page 10

InvesTips: Sukuk: Structure, stability and strategic opportunity  |  September 2026  10

Staying up to date on the latest developments in 
the Sukuk market is important as this space is 
constantly evolving and rapidly expanding. 
Moreover, Islamic finance is not static; Shariah 
interpretations related to Sukuk structures are 
periodically redefined. Staying informed protects 
investors from investing in non -compliant 
options. We look at some of the factors that are 
guiding the future of this landscape:

1 Emerging trends

Green Sukuk issuance is projected to grow materially through 
2030, reflecting the natural convergence of Islamic finance and 
sustainability principles. Key sectors include renewable 
energy, green buildings, low -carbon transportation, water and 
waste management and sustainable agriculture.

Tech advancements  are revolutionising Sukuk origination, 
trading and accessibility. Digitisation offers numerous benefits 
to the Islamic finance landscape: streamlined issuance and 
trading processes, enhanced accessibility through 
tokenisation, immutable audit trails via bl ockchain technology 
and improved secondary market liquidity.

A major regulatory shift is likely to create standardised rules 
across Islamic finance markets globally. The pivotal 
development is AAOIFI's Standard 62, launching in 2026, 
which mandates transparent, Shariah-compliant asset-backing 
and genuine asset ownership. This can fundament ally 
strengthen the Islamic authenticity of Sukuk structures.

The Sukuk market is rapidly expanding beyond its traditional 
Islamic finance strongholds. Non-traditional territories  in 
Africa (e.g., Algeria, Benin and Nigeria), Europe (e.g., 
Luxemburg, UK) and Asia (e.g., Turkey, Pakistan) are 
emerging as Islamic capital market gateways. This expansion 
underscores the evolution of Sukuk from a niche Islamic 
product to a globally reco gnised asset class attracting diverse 
sovereigns and investors across continents.

### Page 11

InvesTips: Sukuk: Structure, stability and strategic opportunity  |  September 2026  11

Conclusion

Ultimately, Sukuk demonstrate that Shariah compliance and sound investment 
strategy are not competing priorities, but complementary ones. By ensuring that 
portfolios are grounded in real economic activity by offering stability through 
tangible asset -backing and transparent structures that channel capital towards 
productive use. Their foundation in ethical, responsible financing appeals to 
investors seeking both resilience and purpose. As global markets increasingly 
prioritise integrity and long-term sustainability, Sukuk stand out as a compelling tool 
for Shariah -compliant investors who want meaningful diversification without 
compromising on their religious and ethical principles.

## Document 484: Who finances private credit in the United States_ What the Federal Reserve’s data reveal (2026-10-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Who finances private credit in the United States_ What the Federal Reserve’s data reveal (2026-10-28).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas

Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

28 October 2026

for a changing

WHO FINANCES PRIVATE CREDIT IN THE UNITED STATES? WHAT THE FEDERAL RESERVE’S DATA REVEAL
Céline Choulet
SOURCE: FEDERAL RESERVE (ENHANCED FINANCIAL ACCOUNTS), BNP PARIBAS
The private credit market raises many questions, and its lack of transparency is not helping. Official public data remain scarce. The available
estimates come mainly from private sources, whose methodologies vary widely. The Financial Accounts of the Federal Reserve (Fed) now shed

new light on the matter. They reveal that private credit finances 7.1% of US corporate debt through households (31% of funds’ total financing),
insurers and pension funds (20%), and banks (19%).
Private credit can take several forms. The most common is loans granted by closed-end funds 1 to small and medium-sized, unlisted and highly
indebted companies. These loans are generally variable-rate, with flexible terms based on cash flow rather than a measure of profitability. While

this asset class has inherent vulnerabilities 2 (Bank of France, 2025), it is also particularly well-suited to financing innovative and long-term
projects (such as digital 3, infrastructure, renewable energy and healthcare), especially when combined with an equity stake in the company. Its
slower growth in Europe likely explains, in part, the region’s lag in innovation financing.
Private credit finances 7.1% of US corporate debt…
Private credit has grown rapidly in the United States over the past decade and has established itself as an alternative to bank loans for

a category of borrowers. It now accounts for 17.3% of the total outstanding loans to American non-financial corporations (NFCs) 4 and 7.1%
1 Strictly speaking, a closed-end fund has a fixed term during which investors cannot withdraw their invested capital. However, the sector has diversified since 2020. The Fed distinguishes
between 1) strictly closed-end private funds (private debt funds), 2) business development companies, which may have an indefinite lifespan and allow for occasional redemptions of

shares, and 3) interval and tender offer funds, which offer the possibility of scheduled redemptions.
2 Notably, high credit risk, high leverage, difficulty in valuing loans, opaque credit ratings, occasionally complex credit structures, high concentration within the sector and increasing
liquidity risk.
3 In 2025, nearly half of the direct loans made by private credit funds went to the tech sector (software, data centres and artificial intelligence) (Abbassi et al., 2026).

4 No comparable data exist for the Eurozone. According to France Invest (2026) data, private credit issued by French funds accounted for 4% of annual bank credit flows to NFCs in 2025.
Who finances US private credit funds?

of their debt. Approximately one-third (35%) is managed by strictly closed-end funds, with another third (36%) by semi-open-end funds
that allow occasional early redemptions. The remaining third is held on the balance sheets of non-resident private debt funds (25%) and
securitisation vehicles (4%).
… through banks, insurers and pension funds, and households

In yet another example of the lengthening of the credit intermediation chain in the United States (following that of the mortgage market),
banks are among the main creditors of private credit funds (65% of their debt and 19% of their total financing). The low risk weighting
of loans granted by banks to these vehicles, compared to that of direct bank loans to non-financial corporations, is undoubtedly a factor
(Chernenko et al., 2024). The holdings of insurers and pension funds – consisting mainly of shares in strictly closed-end funds – make

them among the leading investors in private debt funds (30% of issued shares and 20% of total financing). This asset class offers them the
opportunity to diversify their portfolios and attractive returns. The rise of semi-open-end funds has expanded the investor base to include
retail investors (31% of total financing), albeit at the cost of increased liquidity risk, as evidenced by the significant wave of redemptions in
late 2025 and early 2026 5. American households directly hold 70% of the equity issued by semi-open funds and 22% of the shares issued

by strictly closed funds 6.

## Document 548: ステーブルコインは「国債の買い手」になるのか ～デジタルドルが米国債を支える構図は、日本でも生まれるのか～

Source PDF: all report pdf/ステーブルコインは「国債の買い手」になるのか ～デジタルドルが米国債を支える構図は、日本でも生まれるのか～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Research Report

2026 年 11 月 2 日

1/6 
ステーブルコインは「国債の買い手」になるのか 
～デジタルドルが米国債を支える構図は、日本でも生まれるのか～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  柏村 祐

（要旨） 
○ 米国の巨額の財政赤字や伝統的な買い手の保有縮小により、米国債の「需給懸念」と金利高
止まりが意識される中、暗号資産市場から生まれた「ステーブルコイン」の発行体が新たな
買い手として浮上している。 
○ ステーブルコイン発行体による米国債保有は急増しており、中国の保有減少を一部補う規模
に達している。また、IMF の実証分析でも、ステーブルコインへの需要増加が短期米国債利
回りに低下圧力を及ぼすことが示されている。 
○ 一方、法定通貨担保型ステーブルコインの99.4％は米ドル連動であり、円建ての市場規模は
極めて小さい。当面、円建てステーブルコインが日本国債の需給を支える存在になることは
期待しにくい。 
○ 個人投資家は、米金利を占う上でステーブルコイン市場の動向を新たな金融市場の指標とし
て注視しつつ、国内資産については従来型の金利上昇（債券安）リスクへの備えを継続する
ことが対応策の一つとなる。

1. 米国債の「需給懸念」と金利高止まり～新たな買い手は誰か～ 
米国の金利動向は、世界の金融市場や個人投資家のポートフォリオに多大な影響を及ぼす。足
元では、インフレの高止まりや米連邦準備制度理事会（FRB）の金融政策動向に加え、米国債の
「需給懸念」が長期金利の上昇（債券価格の下落）を招く構造的な要因の一つとして意識されて
いる。米政府の財政赤字が拡大を続ける一方で、米国債の主要な海外保有国の一つである中国
は、外貨準備の多様化などを背景に米国債の保有を減らしつつある。こうした伝統的な買い手の
一部が保有を減らす中、 「一体誰が今後の米国債を買い支えるのか」という問いは、米国株のバリ
ュエーションやドル円相場の行方を左右する論点である。 
こうした中、米国債の新たな買い手として急速に存在感を高めているのが、暗号資産市場から
生まれた「ステーブルコイン」である。法定通貨に価値を連動させる代表的なステーブルコイン
は、その価値を裏付けるために巨額の準備資産を必要とする。 
ステーブルコインは米国債の有力な買い手となり、米国債の利回り上昇を抑える要因になり得

### Page 2

2/6 
るのか。また、デジタルドルが米国債を支えるこの構図は、日本国債と円建てステーブルコイン
の間でも起こり得るのだろうか。本稿では、最近の実証研究を手がかりにこの資金の流れを検証
し、個人投資家にとっての論点を整理する。

2. ステーブルコインは国債の新たな買い手となるか 
(1) 米国債の新たな買い手は誰か 
近年、暗号資産市場の拡大とともに、法定通貨（主に米ドル）に価値を連動させる「ステーブ
ルコイン」の発行残高が拡大している。その発行体は、発行したコインの価値を裏付けるため、
準備資産として安全かつ流動性の高い資産を保有する必要がある。その代表格が短期米国債（米
国財務省短期証券、T-bills）である。 
サンフランシスコ連銀が2026年 9 月に公表したレポートによれば、ステーブルコイン発行体に
よる米国債の保有が急増しており、米国債市場における新たな買い手として浮上している。過去
20年間、米国債の外国人保有比率は低下傾向にあり、特に中国などの外国政府による保有減少が
目立っていた。しかし、ステーブルコイン発行体は過去5年間で米国債関連資産の保有額を約
2,000 億ドル増加させており、これは同時期の中国による米国債保有減少額の4 割超に相当する。
ただし、中国の保有減少は主に長期国債であるのに対し、ステーブルコイン発行体の保有増加は
主に短期米国債であり、満期構成には違いがある。 
資料 1は、中国とステーブルコイン発行体（時価総額上位2銘柄であるUSDTおよびUSDCの発行
体（それぞれTetherおよびCircle））の米国債保有額の推移を示している。足元のトレンドが継続
した場合、ステーブルコイン発行体による短期米国債の需要は、2030年末までに約 4,000 億ドル
へとほぼ倍増するとの試算も示されている。伝統的な買い手が後退するなか、短期ゾーンに限っ
ていえば、ステーブルコイン発行体はすでに主要な買い手の一角を占めつつある。

### Page 3

3/6

資料 1 ステーブルコイン発行体の米国債保有額は急増し、中国の保有減少を一部補う 
（注）ステーブルコイン発行体は時価総額上位 2 社（Tether（USDT）および Circle（USDC））を指す。保有額には米国財務省短期 
証券（T-bills）および現先取引（レポ）を含む。 
（出所）Federal Reserve Bank of San Francisco, “Stablecoin Issuers’ Growing Appetite for Treasury Securities”をもとに第一ラ 
イフ資産運用経済研究所作成

(2) ステーブルコインは国債金利を押し下げるのか 
ステーブルコインによる米国債需要の増加は、実際の金利形成にどのような影響を及ぼすのだ
ろうか。国際通貨基金（IMF）が 2026年3 月に発表したワーキングペーパーは、ステーブルコイ
ンの需要増加が金融市場に与える影響を実証的に分析している。同ペーパーでは、マクロ経済全
体の動き（好景気や株高など）による影響をできるだけ切り分けるため、ステーブルコイン特有
のニュース（規制動向や技術提携など）を手掛かりに、需要の変動を識別する手法（ナラティブ
識別）を用いている。 
資料 2は、この手法を用いて「ステーブルコインの時価総額をピーク時に1％増加させる需要シ
ョック」が生じた際、1カ月物米国債利回りが時間の経過とともにどう反応するか（インパルス応
答）を推計したものである。これを見ると、ショック発生後に利回りは持続的に低下し、約24 週
後に最大で約1.9ベーシスポイント（bp）の押し下げ効果が生じると推計されている。押し下げ
幅そのものは小さいが、ステーブルコインへの需要が国債市場の需給を通じて、短期金利に波及
する経路が実証的に示された意義は小さくない。暗号資産市場の動きが、もはや同市場内の出来

### Page 4

4/6 
事にとどまらなくなっていることを示している。

資料 2 ステーブルコインへの需要増加は短期米国債利回りの低下要因となる 
（注）ステーブルコイン（USDT および USDC）の合計時価総額をピーク時に 1％増加させる需要ショックに対する、1 カ月物米国債 
利回りの推定反応（インパルス応答）。「ナラティブ識別」とは、ニュースなどの定性的な情報をもとに、他の経済要因の影響
を受けにくい出来事を選び、特定のショックを識別する分析手法。青色の実線は推定値、濃い青色と薄い水色の網掛け部分
はそれぞれ 68％および 90％の信頼区間を示す。 
（出所）IMF, “Stablecoin Shocks” (WP/2026/044)をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

(3) 円建てステーブルコインは日本国債の買い手になるか 
米国市場で起きている「デジタルドル（米ドル建てステーブルコイン）が米国債を支える」と
いう構図は、日本でも起きるのだろうか。日本においても、法整備が進み円建てステーブルコイ
ンの発行・流通が本格化すれば、その準備資産として日本国債（特に短期国債）への新たな需要
を生み出す余地はある。 
しかし、現時点では米ドル建てと円建ての市場規模には圧倒的な差が存在する。国際決済銀行
（BIS）が2026年 6 月に公表した「Annual Economic Report 2026」によれば、法定通貨担保型ス
テーブルコインの99.4％が米ドルに連動している。資料3 は、米ドル建て以外のステーブルコイ
ンの時価総額を米ドル建てに対する比率で示したものだが、ユーロ建てやブラジルレアル建てな
どと並び、円建て（JPY）の規模は米ドル建ての0.01％台に過ぎず、極めて小さい水準にとどまっ
ている。 
円建てステーブルコインが日本国債の有力な買い手として市場に影響を与えるようになるに
は、決済や価値保存の手段として円建てステーブルコインのユースケースがグローバルに拡大
し、時価総額が飛躍的に成長することが前提となる。円建ての規模が米ドル建ての1万分の1 程
度にとどまる現状を踏まえれば、当面、円建てステーブルコインが日本国債の需給を左右する局

### Page 5

5/6 
面は想定しにくい。日本の投資家にとっての論点は、円建てではなく、米国市場ですでに起きて
いる変化の側にある。

資料 3 米ドル建て以外のステーブルコインの市場規模は依然として極めて小さい 
（注）米ドル建てステーブルコインの時価総額に対する各通貨建てステーブルコインの時価総額の比率（％）。2026 年 5 月 29 日 
時点。法定通貨担保型ステーブルコインのみを対象とする。 
（出所）BIS, “Annual Economic Report 2026”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

3. 個人投資家へのインプリケーション～「暗号資産×マクロ」の新視点～ 
前節で見てきたように、ステーブルコインの発行体は米国債市場で存在感を高めており、その
需要拡大が短期金利に低下圧力を及ぼすことも実証研究によって示されている。一方で、円建て
ステーブルコインの市場規模は極めて小さく、日本国債の需給に影響を及ぼす存在になるには、
なお市場の大幅な拡大を要する。 
個人投資家にとっての含意は2 点ある。 
第一に、米国の金利動向を占う上で、「ステーブルコイン市場の動向」を新たな金融市場の指標
として注視すべきであるということだ。これまで、暗号資産は伝統的な株式や債券とは異なる投
機的な投資対象と見なされがちだった。しかし、ステーブルコインを介して暗号資産市場の資金
が国債市場に流れ込み、金利形成に影響を与え始めている。暗号資産を保有していない投資家で
あっても、ステーブルコインの時価総額の伸びや、米国で成立したGENIUS法の 2027年 1月の施
行や関連規制の動向が、自身の保有する米国株や米国債券ファンドの価格に影響を及ぼす可能性
がある点に留意が必要だ。 
第二に、日本国内の金利上昇リスクに対する警戒を怠らないことである。米国ではステーブル
コインという「新たな買い手」が国債需給を下支えする構造が生まれつつあるが、日本では当面
同様の需給下支え効果は期待しにくい。日本銀行（日銀）が国債買入れの減額を進め、長年市場
を支えてきた最大の買い手が後退する中、日本国債についても、需給の緩みを通じた金利上昇圧
力には注意が必要である。

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
個人投資家にとっては、ステーブルコインの拡大による米国短期国債の利回り低下を、一つの
市場シナリオとして念頭に置きつつも、国内資産については、保有する債券のデュレーション
（金利変動に対する価格の感応度）を短めに保つなど、従来型の金利上昇（債券安）への備えを
継続することが、対応策の一つとなる。

以 上

景気は弱い動き
中国景気は弱い動きが続いている。9月の製造業PMIは50.1と前月から上昇し、好不況の境目である50を3カ月ぶりに上
回った。構成指数をみると、生産は大きく上昇した一方、就業人員の低下が全体の改善ペースを抑制した。非製造業PMI
は50.2と3カ月ぶりに50を上回ったが、過去と比べると低水準で推移している。なお、2026年4～6月期の実質GDP成長
率は前年比＋4.3%と伸びが鈍化し、季節調整済み前期比（年率換算）も＋3.6%と政府目標の「4.5～5%」を下回った。

固定資産投資は減少、生産は底堅く推移
8月の固定資産投資（年初来累計値）は前年比－7.2%と、前月から減少幅が拡大した。情報サービス業などハイテク産
業の投資は増加したものの、不動産開発投資やインフラ投資を中心に幅広い分野で低迷している。
8月の工業生産は前年比＋5.2%と増加し、底堅く推移している。リチウムイオン電池（前年比＋57.2%）や工業用ロボット
（同＋34.6%）が大幅に増加するなど、技術集約型のハイテク製造業（同＋16.7%）が全体を牽引した。

消費は弱い動き、物価は低迷
8月の小売売上高（名目）は前年比＋0.4%と、前月から増加幅が縮小した。需要が強く、価格上昇も顕著な通信機器（前
年比＋27.3%）は大幅な増加が続いた一方、政策効果の剥落や不動産不況を背景に自動車（同－18.5%）や建築・リ
フォーム材料（同－11.8%）の減少が大きかった。なお、季節調整済み前月比は－0.13%と、3カ月ぶりにマイナスだった。
8月の消費者物価は前年比＋0.8%と、中東情勢の混乱に伴う物価押し上げ効果が和らぎ、前月に続き低い伸びとなった。

為替は元高、株価は下落
人民元の対ドルレート（9月末）は1ドル＝6.71元だった。大幅な貿易黒字に加え、米国政治・経済の先行き不透明感を背
景としたドルへの慎重な見方が根強く、ドル安・元高が進んだ。8月末の外貨準備高は3兆4,383億ドルと増加した。
9月の上海総合株価指数は下落した。月中旬まで横ばいで推移したが、月末の米中首脳会談の成果が市場の期待を下
回ったことから株価は下落し、約2カ月ぶりの安値を記録した。業種別指数は、材料やエネルギーの下落が大きかった。

6 Mitsubishi UFJ Research and Consulting
9月の米中首脳会談は一定の成果も、中心は対話継続の確認
9月24日、米国・ワシントンで米中首脳会談が実施された。5月の会談で打ち出された「建設的戦略安定関係」の構築を再
確認したほか、年内あと2回の会談の可能性が示唆されるなど、首脳間の対話の継続が確認された（下図）。
具体的には、米中双方が300億ドル相当の品目で関税引き下げに合意したほか、AI分野で対話枠組みを構築する方針で
合意するなど、個別分野では一定の進展が得られた。もっとも、総じてみると内容は概ね想定内であり、米中対立の全面
的な解消には至っていない。貿易戦争の休戦も今年11月までとされた期限が2カ月延長されたにとどまっており、今後も
両者間で対話を継続することを確認したことが最大の成果だったといえよう。
2026年9月の米中首脳会談における主な内容
（出所） 各種報道より当社作成
主な成果 内容
対話維持と関係安定化 5月に打ち出した「建設的戦略安定関係」を再確認年内あと2回の会談の可能性（11月APEC、12月G20）

貿易 一部関税引き下げと休戦延長 5月に設置した米中貿易委員会の運用を開始相互に300億ドル相当の非機微品目で関税引き下げに合意関税・輸出管理などを巡る休戦を2027年1月10日まで延長
台湾問題 明確な合意なし 中国は米国に台湾独立への「反対」明言を要求（現在は「支持しない」）米国側発表に政策変更や合意の記載なし
歴史認識 中国の思惑に沿う発表 両首脳が「米中は第2次世界大戦の同盟国」であったことを確認

輸出価格は上昇も、デフレ輸出への懸念はなお強い
中国の輸出価格が足元で上昇している（左図）。2023年以降、EVや太陽光パネル、鉄鋼を中心に輸出価格は下落傾向
にあり、廉価品の輸出が海外市場を席巻する「デフレ輸出」が国際問題となったが、2026年4月以降、状況が一変した。品
目別でみると、世界的に需給が逼迫している集積回路やPC、PC部品の価格上昇が顕著である。
もっとも、足元の価格上昇は一部の品目に偏っている。HSコード4桁で分類した全1,204品目のうち、足元でも5割近くで価
格が下落している（右図）。このため、デフレ輸出への懸念は依然として根強い。

輸出価格 輸出価格が前年から上昇／下落した品目の割合

1.   资本市场新闻 
央行：将于11月3日开展证券、基金、保险公司互换便利操作，申请额度超过1000
亿元 
为支持资本市场健康稳定发展，中国人民银行11月2日发布互换便利（SFISF）招
标公告〔2026〕第1号，将于11月3日开展证券、基金、保险公司互换便利操作，互
换期限1年，可视情展期。截至11月2日共有31家证券、基金公司申报需求，申请额
度超过1000亿元。互换便利是央行支持资本市场的重要结构性货币政策工具，通过“以
券换券”方式提升证券、基金、保险公司的流动性与股票增持能力，有助于增强资本市
场内在稳定性，提振市场信心。 
金融法草案二审稿充实金融消费者、投资者保护内容，并对互联网营销等活动增加
针对性规定 
《中华人民共和国金融法（草案） 》11月2日提请十四届全国人大常委会第二十五
次会议二次审议。草案二审稿充实金融消费者、投资者保护内容：提供金融产品和服务
应当设定公平合理的交易条件，不得通过格式条款作出排除或者限制金融消费者、投资
者权利等不公平、不合理的规定；金融产品和服务的营销主体应当加强客户适当性管理，
充分提示风险，提供与客户状况相匹配的金融产品和服务。同时，草案二审稿对互联网
营销等活动增加针对性规定：通过互联网提供或者营销金融产品和服务，应当遵守本法
和有关法律、行政法规及专门规定；对非公开发行的金融产品和服务，不得通过互联网
等方式进行广告宣传、公开劝诱或者变相公开劝诱。此外，草案二审稿增加金融工作坚
持市场化法治化方向、推进国际金融中心建设的内容，并对资产管理业务、跨境金融活
动监管等增加相关规定。 
2.   行业新闻 
韩国总统李在明公布该国史上规模最大的预算案，承诺“大力投资人工智能及未来
增长引擎” 
韩国总统李在明11月2日在国会发表施政演说，公布2027年度预算案，总支出
820.9万亿韩元，同比增长12.8%，规模与增幅均创历史新高，承诺大力投资人工智能及
未来增长引擎。其中，21.3万亿韩元投向半导体制造集群、AI数据中心、实体人工智能
三大超大型项目，规模接近2026年的两倍；13.8万亿韩元用于培育接替半导体的“NEXT
十大战略技术领域” ；4.7万亿韩元支持前沿人工智能开发计划；研发预算总额达到创纪
录的39.5万亿韩元。 
辽宁省：到2030年全省信息通信业整体水平全面提升，建成覆盖完善、性能领先
的新一代通信网

### Page 5

5 / 8 
经辽宁省政府常务会审议通过，辽宁省通信管理局正式发布《辽宁省信息通信行业
发展“十五五”规划》 。 《规划》围绕加快新型信息基础设施建设布局、提升行业监管和
服务现代化水平、增强网络与数据安全保障能力、全面推进各领域数智化融合应用4个
部分，部署了17项重点任务及7项专栏工程，构建涵盖行业发展、绿色发展、能力建
设、应用普及共13项量化指标体系。 《规划》提出，到2030年全省信息通信业整体水
平全面提升，建成覆盖完善、性能领先的新一代通信网，算力基础设施供给能力显著增
强，行业融合赋能水平进一步提升，为到2035年基本实现信息通信行业现代化奠定坚
实基础。

4.   公司公告 
天力复合:股东减持股份结果公告 
公司股东西部金属材料股份有限公司（控股股东）及樊科社、孙昊、吴江涛、庞国
庆、何波等高级管理人员，于2026年8月至10月期间以集中竞价方式合计减持公司股
份1,448,855股，占公司总股本的1.33%，减持价格区间为30.09-48.41元/股，已减持总
金额合计约 5,051.65万元，本次减持计划实施完毕。其中，控股股东西部材料减持
1,089,000股，占总股本的1.00%，减持后持股比例降至43.27%。本次减持事项与股东
此前已披露的计划、承诺一致，实际减持数量与计划的差异系相关股东自主决策所致，
不会导致公司控股股东及实际控制人发生变化。

### Page 8

免责及评级说明部分

东吴证券投资评级标准 
投资评级基于分析师对报告发布日后 6至 12个月内行业或公司回报潜力相对基准表现
的预期（A 股市场基准为沪深 300 指数，香港市场基准为恒生指数，美国市场基准为标普 
500 指数，新三板基准指数为三板成指（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针对做市转
让标的），北交所基准指数为北证 50指数），具体如下： 
公司投资评级： 
买入：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在 15%以上； 
增持：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于 5%与 15%之间； 
中性：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-5%与 5%之间； 
减持：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-15%与-5%之间； 
卖出：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在-15%以下。 
行业投资评级： 
增持： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对强于基准 5%以上； 
中性： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对基准-5%与 5%； 
减持： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对弱于基准 5%以上。 
我们在此提醒您，不同证券研究机构采用不同的评级术语及评级标准。我们采用的是相
对评级体系，表示投资的相对比重建议。投资者买入或者卖出证券的决定应当充分考虑自身

### Page 3

原油
(株)日本総合研究所 原油市場展望 2026年11月
◆現状：100ドルを挟んで乱高下
10月のＷＴＩ原油先物価格は 、上旬に90ドル
前後で方向感に欠ける展開。米国の中東への増派
が価格を押し上げた一方 、Ｇ７による石油備蓄放
出の合意が価格を下押し。
中旬には、一時110ドル台乗せ 。イエメンの武装
組織フーシがサウジアラビアの空港や石油関連施設
を攻撃したほか 、イランがホルムズ海峡周辺を航行
する船舶を攻撃したことで 、中東からの原油供給が
停滞するとの懸念が強まったことが主因。
下旬にかけては、100ドルを挟んで一進一退。フー
シとサウジアラビアの間で攻撃の応酬が激化するなか、
米国とイスラエルが11月の米中間選挙後にイランへ
の攻撃を再開するとの報道が価格上昇圧力に 。一
方、ロシアとウクライナの和平交渉が再開するとの期
待が高まったことが価格下落圧力に。
◆投機筋のネットポジションは小幅に縮小
投機筋の買い越し幅は緩やかに縮小 。中東情勢
を巡る不透明感は根強いものの 、いずれ事態が安

定に向かうと見込む投資家が増加している可能性。
◆見通し：当面100ドル前後で高止まり
中東における戦闘がこれ以上激化しないことを前
提とするメイン シナリオでは、原油価格が100ドルを
中心とする高値圏で推移する見通し。ホルムズ海峡
の通航制約が価格を下支えする一方 、パイプライン
などの代替ルートを用いた中東からの輸出増が上値
を抑えると予想。

### Page 4

原油
(株)日本総合研究所 原油市場展望 2026年11月3
◆米国の増産ペースは緩やか
米国では、原油生産量が増加。７～９月期の生
産量は日量1,400万バレル弱と、過去最高水準に。
南部に位置するパーミアン地区 （テキサス・ニューメ
キシコ州など）における増産が全体を押し上げ。
もっとも、増産ペースは緩やか。足元の生産量は、
中東危機前の2025年10～12月期対比＋0.3％
（日量４万バレル ）の増加にとどまる状況 。シェー
ル革命後の10年代後半や、コロナ禍からの持ち直
し局面であった20年代前半と比べると、増勢は鈍化。
◆既存油井の減耗と設備投資の抑制が背景
原油高によって米石油企業の収益環境が改善し
ているにもかかわらず、増産ペースが緩やかにとどまる
背景として、次の２点が指摘可能。
第１に、既存油井の減耗 。パーミアン地区では 、
既存油井の減退ペースが速く 、新規油井による増
産分をほぼ相殺している状況。過去局面並みの増
産量を得るためには、かつてよりも多くの油井を掘削
する必要があり、その分コストが増大。

第２に、設備投資の抑制。パーミアン地区を管轄
するダラス連銀の調査によると 、生産量全体の８割
以上を占める大企業の半数は、増加したフリーキャッ
シュフローの主な使途として株主還元を選択 。設備
投資を選んだ企業は約２割どまり。近年の米国では、
政権交代の度に環境・エネルギー政策が転換するな
ど、政策面の不確実性が高く 、これが石油企業によ
る積極的な増産投資を抑制している可能性。
トピック：米国の増産を制約する油井の減耗と設備投資の抑制

日本総合研究所主催 シンポジウムのご案内
米中対立の常態化や米国の自国第一主義の強まりを背景に、既存の国際経済秩序が揺らいでいます。こうしたなか、各国は存
在感を高めるグローバルサウス諸国との外交・経済関係の強化を急いでいます。日本も、OECD（経済協力開発機構）などと協調し
ながら、関係強化を目指していますが、現状はASEANやインドなどに偏重しており、アフリカや中南米との連携は限定的です。本シン
ポジウムでは、はじめに世界経済の構造変化や日本のグローバルサウス諸国との連携策の現状・課題を多角的に検証します。その
上で、日本がグローバルサウス諸国との関係を深化させるための具体的な戦略を議論します。

王翊安 
wade.wang@ctbcsis.com

電子上游-銅箔基板CCL 
台光電 (2383 TT)

重新覆蓋  
買進(Buy) 
需求明確，擴產延伸至 2030 年  
⚫ 漲價延續加 M9放量，產品組合持續優化  
⚫ ASIC與衛星天上板放量，推升高階材料需求  
⚫ 客戶主動簽訂 LTA，擴產規劃延伸至 2030年 
3Q26獲利優於預期：台光電(2383)3Q26 營收 608.9億元(QoQ+28.8%，
YoY+142.1%)，毛利率37.7%(QoQ+3.8ppts，YoY+7.6ppts)，EPS為40.47
元，皆創歷史新高，也優於前次公司財測及市場預期。成長主要來自
2Q26 漲價效益延續，加上產品組合優化， M7 以上營收占比提升至
65~70%。展望 4Q26，我們認為玻纖布、高階銅箔等上游原物料供給持
續偏緊，漲價將延續到 4Q26。同時 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 放量後，M9營
收占比將逐步提高，帶動整體產品組合往 M9移動。M9毛利率在 50%
以上，有助於整體毛利率持續上行；預計 2027年M9營收占比達雙位
數。我們預估 4Q26營收 712.4 億元(QoQ+17.0%，YoY+185.8%)，毛利
率40.8%，EPS為52.08元。  
ASIC 客戶放量，帶動 M7/M8 占比持續提升： 除了 NVIDIA，AWS 
Trainium 3 與Google TPU v8 放量也將帶動營收成長，並推升 M7/M8
營收占比。我們預估 2027 M7/M8營收佔比將達77%。此外，兩大ASIC
客戶後續的 PCB規格都將採用更高階數的厚板 HDI，以及導入 n+m不
對稱壓合設計，板廠加工段的良率將面臨挑戰。對 CCL廠來說，良率
損耗代表在既有出貨量之外，還需要額外的材料用量。加上 TPU 與
Trainium 2027 年晶片出貨量分別上看 900萬顆與 300萬顆以上，我們
認為高階 CCL需求將進一步放大 。 
觀音廠產能遞延，長線領導地位不變： 公司產能本次調整為 2026~2030
年底月產能分別為 585/870/1,045/1,195/1,525 萬張。觀音廠因台灣營建
缺料導致土建延後，加上消防驗收程序拉長，原訂 12M26開出的 30萬
張延後半年至 2H27，原訂 2H27開出的 60萬張延至 2029年。不過崑
山廠 120萬張有望提前自 12M26起分階段量產，部分彌補觀音廠的遞
延。我們認為雖出現產能遞延， 長期需求 趨勢不變。我們預估
2026/2027/2028 年 EPS 分別為 135.00/292.26/479.16 元，以 2027 年
EPS×25 倍PER推估目標價為 7,300元，建議買進。  
風險因子：上游缺料導致出貨瓶頸 、CSP資本支出放緩 。

個股報告  
                                       2 0 2 6 / 10 /29

2 
 公司簡介：台光電成立於 1992年，總部位於桃園觀音，專注於銅箔基板 (CCL)及黏合片(PP)的研發與製造。生產基地包括台灣觀音、中國崑山、中山、黃
石及馬來西亞檳城， 3Q26營收以基礎設施 (Infra)占 75~80%為最大宗，行動
裝置占 10~15%，車用/工業/其他占 5~10%。2025年公司 CCL市占率達 16.2%，
位居全球第一，其中高速材料市占率達 38.6%。產品全數自行研發，以平台
化方案供應 AI 伺服器、交換器、低軌衛星及載板材料等應用。目前公司月
產能 585萬張。  
圖表一、台光電營收產品組合趨勢

資料來源： Bloomberg、中信投顧彙整

SpaceX V3 放量，台光電重心轉向天上板： SpaceX V3 將於 2027 年開始放
量。除了地面板，台光電預計可取得衛星天上板大部分的份額，天上板也是
公司後續出貨重點。根據我們供應鏈調查，台燿已通過 V3地面板驗證，將
成為二供，我們認為這代表台光電未來將以毛利較高的天上板為主軸，地面
板出貨量預計維持在 2026 年水準，後續衛星材料的增量主要來自天上板。
天上板材料需使用極低氣體排放 (low outgassing) 樹脂，關鍵技術在材料廠，
且終端客戶驗證期較長，進入門檻較高，毛利表現應優於地面板。 V3 預計
2H27開始大量發射，板廠出貨約提前 2~3季，我們預估 4Q26起將看到衛星
業務帶來的增量。目前衛星材料以 M7為主，公司預期 2027年將升級至 M8，
有助於推升單價。我們預估 2027年LEO營收達 322.3 億元(YoY+158.4%)。

載板材料認證推進，有望成為 2027/2028 年獲利增量： 公司載板材料主要切
入 BT載板，市占率由 2025 年的 0.4%提升至 2026 年的 1.6%。目前有新專
案進行中，持續進行客戶認證，預計 2026年底有結果；若通過，市占率可望
進一步提升，公司目標未來三年載板 CCL市占率達 5%。產能方面，崑山廠
目前有 30萬張/月載板產能， 2H27中山、觀音、崑山廠各增加 30萬張/月，
2027 年底載板產能將達 120萬張/月。我們預估載板材料毛利率可達 50%，
高於公司平均，可望成為 2027/2028 年獲利結構的潛在增量。

資料來源：中信投顧整理  
客戶主動要求 LTA，產業能見度延長至 2030 年：本次法說會公司給出更長
的 CCL 產業能見度，預期需求將維持強勁至 2029~2030 年，並首次提出
2029~2030 年擴產規劃，公司原規劃 2026~2028 年底月產能為 615/930/1,110
萬張，本次調整為 2026~2030 年底月產能分別為 585/870/1,045/1,195/1,525
萬張。新增擴產主要是因應客戶主動提出的長期合約 (LTA)，客戶要求的量已
超過公司原規劃的產能增加量。目前簽訂的 LTA至少 3年，雖客戶沒有預付
款，但合約只鎖量不鎖價，後續仍會依市況調整價格。我們認為這對整體產
業是更正面的訊號，代表下游客戶預期高階 CCL 中長期供給仍偏緊，也提
高了產業擴產與營收的能見度。

## Document 676: 国内官方2026年10月PMI数据解读

Source PDF: all report pdf/国内官方2026年10月PMI数据解读.pdf
Report source: 浙商期货
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

国内官方2026年10月PMI数据解读（2026-10-31）
期货研究报告
2026-10-31
数据截至2026-10
本报告基于国家统计局、中国物流与采购联合会 2026-10-31 发布的 2026 年 10 月中国采购经理指数。
制造业PMI
49.3%

非制造业PMI
49.7%
环比 -0.5
降至临界点下方
综合PMI产出指数
49.7%
环比 -1.0
企业生产经营总体放缓
一、极简版速读
综合结论：10月三大PMI指数全面回落，经济景气水平整体回落、再度全面落入收缩区间，但装备制造业、高技术制造业新动能延续扩张并托底供需，
方向上维持“政策托底+新动能支撑”的基本盘判断。
制造业景气明显回调：制造业PMI 49.3%，较上月回落0.8个百分点，再度落入收缩区间，主要受“十一”假期前需求提前释放、国际环境复杂多变影
响。
结构特征供需放缓：供需两端同步放缓（生产49.8、新订单49.4），大中小企业分化加大（大型50.2扩张、中小均低于临界点），价格增势高位放缓，

库存处主动去库尾声、补库动能不足。
非制造业同步走弱：商务活动49.7%，服务业50.0%临界点附近、建筑业48.0%明显回落，金融业景气回落幅度较大。
浙商期货宏观研究团队
浙商期货有限公司期货交易咨询业务资格：证监许可[2011]1287号
撰写人：朱枭鹏｜咨询证号：Z0015158｜联系方式：0571-87219355

### Page 2

整体展望：政策托底与新动能支撑下，预计11月制造业具备稳定基础，但回升空间相对有限，需求不足仍是主要约束。
图1：制造业PMI&非制造业PMI（近一年）
二、制造业PMI总体判断与核心特征

### Page 3

2.1 总体判断
10月制造业PMI为49.3%（环比-0.8pct），连续两个月扩张后再度落入收缩区间，制造业景气水平明显回调。从企业规模看，大型企业PMI为50.2%
（-0.4pct），连续保持扩张、运行相对稳健；中型企业48.4%（-1.3pct）、小型企业47.5%（-1.4pct）均低于临界点且回落明显，中小企业供需两端
承压。
企业规模 本月值 环比变动 判断
大型企业 50.2% -0.4 仍处扩张区间
中型企业 48.4% -1.3 低于临界点
小型企业 47.5% -1.4 低于临界点
本月主矛盾：大中小企业分化再度放大——大型企业“稳中有增”，中小型企业“波动偏弱”，结构性分化是拖累制造业整体景气回落的主因。

### Page 4

图2：制造业PMI：大中小企业（近一年）
2.2 核心特征
① 供需关系
生产指数49.8%（-1.9pct）、新订单指数49.4%（-1.1pct）：经济活动动能指标（生产-新订单）+0.4pct，供给仍略强于需求，供强需弱格局延续。
生产端前期较快扩张势头在需求回调带动下放缓，但指数仅略低于临界点，生产活动基本平稳。内外需方面：新出口订单48.7%（-1.3pct），国外圣诞

### Page 5

订单上月集中释放后回落，外需边际走弱；结构上装备制造业新订单回升1个百分点至51%以上（“六张网”实物工作量注入动能）、高技术制造业需求
保持扩张，新动能对需求形成托底。
图3：制造业PMI：生产&新订单&新出口订单指数（近一年）
② 价格与利润

### Page 6

主要原材料购进价格56.8%（-4.0pct）、出厂价格51.7%（-2.3pct）：价格增势高位放缓，但仍处偏高水平（两者均高于去年同期4个百分点以上）。
“购进-出厂”价差5.1pct（上月6.8pct，收窄1.7pct），中下游利润挤压边际缓解；两指数同步回落显示成本端驱动减弱、需求端价格动能放缓，短
期成本推动型通胀压力降温。
图4：制造业PMI：主要原材料购进价格&出厂价格指数（近一年）
③ 库存周期

### Page 7

产成品库存47.5%（-0.1pct）、原材料库存48.4%（+0.2pct）、采购量48.9%（-2.1pct）：产成品库存持平略降、原材料库存微升、采购量明显回
落，反映企业补库意愿不足、生产采购趋于谨慎，整体处于主动去库向被动去库过渡阶段，补库周期尚未启动，与需求偏弱相互印证。
图5：制造业PMI：产成品库存&原材料库存&采购量（近一年）
三、非制造业PMI总体判断与核心特征

### Page 8

3.1 总体判断
10月非制造业商务活动指数49.7%（-0.5pct），降至临界点下方，非制造业景气水平有所下降，扩张态势中断。综合PMI产出指数49.7%
（-1.0pct），我国企业生产经营活动总体放缓。
3.2 核心特征
① 服务业：景气水平临界点附近、内部结构分化。服务业商务活动指数50.0%（-0.2pct），“十一”假期相关行业（住宿餐饮、旅游等）有所回升，
但资本市场服务、保险、房地产等行业商务活动指数低于临界点，金融行业商务活动指数回落且幅度较大，是拖累非制造业的主要分项。
② 建筑业：扩张区间明显回落。建筑业商务活动指数48.0%（-2.3pct），由上月的扩张区间落入收缩区间，施工活动有所放缓；地产链景气偏弱与基
建实物工作量的阶段性节奏对建筑业形成拖累，后续需关注专项债放量对基建施工的带动。

### Page 9

图6：非制造业：综合&服务业&建筑业（近一年）
四、综合PMI与核心结论
4.1 综合PMI产出指数

### Page 10

10月综合PMI产出指数49.7%（-1.0pct），制造业（49.3%）与非制造业（49.7%）双双低于临界点，三大指数全面处于收缩区间，反映出当前经济景
气整体回落、企业产能利用与生产经营活动总体放缓。
4.2 核心结论
制造业：景气回调、供需同步放缓，需求不足、供强需弱仍是核心矛盾；大型企业稳健扩张、新动能（装备制造、高技术制造）供需延续扩张提供结构
性支撑，价格增势高位放缓缓解中下游利润压力，库存仍处去库尾声。
非制造业：金融业与房地产景气回落、建筑业扩张中断是主要拖累，服务业假期相关行业回升但整体仅临界点附近，非制造业动能整体偏弱。
展望：政策面“六张网”实物工作量、专项债集中放量（加快形成实物工作量并带动装备、钢铁等供需释放）与11月年尾冲量、电商促销集中释放消
费，预计11月制造业具备稳定基础；但随气温下降部分行业转淡，制造业整体回升空间或相对有限，需求不足仍需宏观政策加大逆周期调节力度予以对
冲，重点关注增量政策落地节奏与地产链修复情况。
免责声明
此报告所载的全部内容仅作参考之用。此报告的内容不构成对任何人的投资建议，且浙商期货有限公司（以下简称“本公司”）不会因接收人收到此报

告所含信息可在不发出通知的情形下做出修改，投资者应当自行关注相应的更新或修改。

[Table_Tag]    [Table_Summary] 观点 
◼ 周度ECI指数：从周度数据来看，截至2026年11月1日，本周ECI供
给指数为50.19%，较上周环比持平；ECI需求指数为49.93%，较上周
环比持平。从分项来看，ECI投资指数为49.97%，较上周环比回升0.02
个百分点；ECI消费指数为49.74%，较上周环比回落0.01个百分点；
ECI出口指数为50.14%，较上周环比回落0.01个百分点。 
◼ 月度ECI指数：从10月整月的高频数据来看，ECI供给指数为50.19%，
较9月回升0.03个百分点；ECI需求指数为49.94%，较9月环比持平。
从分项来看，ECI投资指数为49.97%，较9月回落0.01个百分点；ECI
消费指数为49.76%，较9月回落0.01个百分点；ECI出口指数为50.16%，
较9月回落0.01个百分点。 
◼ 经济高频数据： 
➢ 生产：10月工业生产保持平稳运行。受到长假期节后复工较慢的影响，
10月PMI生产指数回落至荣枯线以下，但主要行业开工率保持平稳运
行态势。结构上仍旧呈现上游行业景气度较高，而下游行业偏弱的特征，
汽车轮胎开工率和PTA开工率均低于去年同期水平。 
➢ 消费：人均旅游支出较去年同期有所放缓。文化和旅游部发布数据显示，
2026年前三季度，国内居民出游人次53.07亿，比上年同期增加3.09
亿，同比增长6.2%。国内居民出游总花费4.95万亿元，比上年增加0.11
万亿元，同比增长2.2%。据此测算，2026年前三季度人均出游支出约
为932.7元，同比回落3.7%，但边际来看服务消费仍保持较高景气度。 
➢ 地产：10月二手房销售明显改善。本周（10月24日至10月30日）主
要城市新房销售有所改善、二手房销售有所回落，其中三十大中城市商
品房成交面积录得164.6万平方米，环比回升6.3%，而18城二手房商
品房成交面积录得254.9万平方米，环比回落7.5%。从10月整体来看，
二手房销售景气度明显优于新房，10月30大中城市商品房成交面积同
比录得-18.8%，而18城二手房成交面积同比录得11.7%。 
➢ 出口：“抢出口”或对四季度出口形成支撑。受中秋国庆连假的影响，9
月出现了出口前置特征，即10月份出口或因此受到部分扰动。而展望
整个四季度来看，在2027年电池出口退税取消、新能源汽车出口退税
率降低、欧盟对我国混动车出口设置限额等因素的影响下，四季度或出
现一定的“抢出口”特征。 
➢ 物价：10月PPI同比增速或延续回升。国内方面，10月猪肉和蔬菜价
格延续季节性回落，其中本周蔬菜价格录得2022年6月以来当周新低；
国际方面，10月份油价环比继续上行，且去年同期油价基数较低，预计
10月PPI同比读数将较9月进一步上行。 
➢ 整体来看，尽管10月份PMI数据受到中秋国庆连假影响有所回落，但
经济修复的结构性特征仍旧明显，工业生产和出口仍保持较高景气度，
二手房销售也受政策提振明显回暖，四季度经济增速有望较三季度进一
步回升。而从经济增长的中长期结构变化来看，目前地产链条贡献正逐
步下降，出口升级、服务消费和非地产投资正在成为新的稳定器，即我
国也正经历“去地产化增长”。 
◼ 风险提示：美国货币政策仍有不确定性；政策出台力度低于市场预期；
房地产改善的持续性待观察。

2.   本周高频数据概览 
2.1.   工业生产：10月份工业生产仍保持较高景气度 
开工率方面， 本周主要行业开工率多数回升。 其中本周汽车全/半钢胎开工率为57.79%
和64.78%，分别环比回升0.09个百分点和环比持平；本周PTA开工率录得69.01%，环
比回升1.38个百分点，较2025年同期回落8.68个百分点；本周钢厂高炉开工率录得
80.01%，环比回落0.39个百分点，较2025年同期回落1.72个百分点。 
库存和产量方面，本周六港口炼焦煤库存合计315.29万吨，环比回升17.59万吨；
本周主要钢厂建筑钢材库存录得199.06万吨，环比回落10.29万吨；本周建筑钢材企业
螺纹钢产量录得181.18万吨，环比回升6.45万吨。 
负荷率方面，10月23日沿海七省电厂负荷率均值录得74.57%，环比回落0.57个
百分点，较2025年同期回落1.43个百分点。

2.2.   消费：服务消费景气度与去年同期基本持平 
乘用车消费方面，10月25日乘用车当周日均销量录得50889辆，较2025年同期
回落18382辆。根据乘联会发布的最新数据，10月1-25日乘用车市场零售录得114.5万
辆，同比2025年同期下降26.0%，环比上月同期持平，其中新能源车市场零售录得75.3
万辆，同比2025年同期下降13.0%，环比上月同期下降1.0%。 
人员流动方面，本周航班执飞率均值为84.79%，环比回升0.01个百分点，较2025
年同期回落0.96个百分点。地铁日均客运量录得7704.03万人，环比回落369.97万人，
较2025年同期回落530.55万人。 
票房方面，本周票房收入录得1.81亿元，环比回落0.38亿元，较2025年同期回落
0.37亿元；本周观影人次录得497.53万人，环比回落97.49万人，较2025年同期回落
90.25万人。

2.3.   投资：水泥发运率优于去年同期 
基建投资方面，2026年10月28日石油沥青装置开工率录得23.90%，环比回升1.30
个百分点，较2025年同期回落7.60个百分点；2026年10月23日全国水泥发运率录得
43.13%，环比回落0.31个百分点，较2025年同期回升5.98个百分点。2026年10月30
日螺纹钢和建材表观需求分别录得205.99万吨和291.19万吨，分别环比回升4.99万吨
和8.52万吨。 
房地产投资方面，上周100大中城市供应土地占地面积录得1753.71万平方米，环

2026/10/30 央行   
为进一步优化短端利率调控机制，从2026年11月2日起，公
开市场操作进行如下调整：一、每个工作日开展隔夜逆回购操
作招标，采用固定利率、数量招标。目前操作利率为1.25%。
二、7天期逆回购操作调整为固定数量、利率招标、多重价位中
标，主要在税期、月末等时点开展。三、原在7天期逆回购操
作利率基础上加减点的其他工具，包括临时正/逆回购、境外央
行类机构回购工具等，均调整为在隔夜逆回购操作利率基础上
加减点，加减点幅度不变。

东吴证券投资评级标准 
投资评级基于分析师对报告发布日后 6至 12个月内行业或公司回报潜力相对基准表现
的预期（A 股市场基准为沪深 300 指数，香港市场基准为恒生指数，美国市场基准为标普 
500 指数，新三板基准指数为三板成指（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针对做市转
让标的），北交所基准指数为北证 50指数），具体如下： 
公司投资评级： 
买入：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在 15%以上； 
增持：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于 5%与 15%之间； 
中性：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-5%与 5%之间； 
减持：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-15%与-5%之间； 
卖出：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在-15%以下。 
行业投资评级： 
增持： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对强于基准 5%以上； 
中性： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对基准-5%与 5%； 
减持： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对弱于基准 5%以上。 
我们在此提醒您，不同证券研究机构采用不同的评级术语及评级标准。我们采用的是相
对评级体系，表示投资的相对比重建议。投资者买入或者卖出证券的决定应当充分考虑自身
特定状况，如具体投资目的、财务状况以及特定需求等，并完整理解和使用本报告内容，不
应视本报告为做出投资决策的唯一因素。

## Document 788: 监管生变？加密股10月承压今日反弹，美国中选或改写特朗普时代金融市场格局

Source PDF: all report pdf/监管生变？加密股10月承压今日反弹，美国中选或改写特朗普时代金融市场格局.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Coinbase 10 ⽉股价重挫 23% ， Galaxy Digital ⼤跌 30% ，⽬前加密相关股票的看跌保护成
本攀升⾄数⽉⾼点。市场担⼼若⺠主党同时拿下参众两院，加密及预测市场的监管环境将
⼤概率趋严，特朗普及其关联⼈⼠也可能⾯临⼀波国会调查。周⼆美国中选开始投票后，
Coinbase 涨 6% ， Galaxy Digital ⼤涨 13% 。
摘要：
特朗普执政以来形成的数字⾦融监管红利，正⾯临中期选举的直接冲击。随着美国选⺠在周⼆
⾛进投票站，部分投资者已开始提前为⺠主党胜选后的监管收紧布局。
期权市场数据显⽰，加密相关股票的看跌保护成本持续攀升。Coinbase 未来⼀个⽉下跌 10% 的
保护成本，已升⾄ 4 ⽉以来最⾼⽔平； Strategy 的同类指标则处于 6 ⽉以来峰值。
与此同时，追踪加密关联股票的两只 ETF——Global X Blockchain ETF 与 Amplify Blockchain
Technology ETF—— 上⽉均出现资⾦净流出。

市场担忧的核⼼逻辑在于：若⺠主党同时拿下参众两院，加密及预测市场的监管环境将⼤概率
趋严，特朗普及其关联⼈⼠也可能⾯临⼀波国会调查。
StoneX Financial 分析师 Mark Palmer 表⽰， " 市场已意识到，加密公司从 SEC 和 CFTC 监管调整
中获得的部分红利并⾮永久性的，其⾛向取决于选举结果。 " 他指出，尽管选举⻛险已有所消
化，但若⺠主党以较⼤优势拿下众议院，加密股周三可能出现负⾯的即时反应。
加密板块的压⼒已在 10 ⽉数据中有所体现。 Coinbase 当⽉股价重挫 23% ， Galaxy Digital 下跌
30% ，均为 2022 年以来最差单⽉表现； Strategy 和 Bullish 分别下跌 15% 和 32% 。周⼆美国开始
投票后，上述个股有所反弹， Coinbase 涨 6% ， Galaxy Digital ⼤涨 13% 。
《清晰法案》受阻，⽴法前景蒙阴
9 ⽉，⺠主党联合少数共和党议员阻⽌了《清晰法案》（ Clarity Act ）的推进，该法案原计划将数

字资产⾏业的主要监管权移交商品期货交易委员会（ CFTC ）。法案受阻后， Coinbase 和 Circle
Internet Group 等股票随即⼤幅下跌。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

Binance 遭遇史上最⼤⿊客攻击
加密⾏业在 10 ⽉下旬还遭遇了另⼀重打击。⿊客从加密交易所 Binance 盗取 63 亿美元，创下加密
史上规模最⼤的盗窃事件。
摩根⼠丹利分析师 Michael Cyprys 在客⼾报告中写道，这起⾼调的⿊客事件可能引发 " 对⽹络安
全、资产托管、运营保障及客⼾资产保护的更严格监管审视 " 。
Coinbase 、 Bullish 和 Circle 等加密公司近年来积极拓展代币化和预测市场等新业务，以降低对单
纯加密交易的依赖，但其营收来源⽬前仍以加密业务为主，监管不确定性的影响难以完全规
避。
⺠主党胜选或利好传统交易所
若⺠主党取得多数席位，受益者可能并⾮加密新贵，⽽是 Cboe Global Markets 和 CME Group 等
成熟交易所。
分析⼈⼠认为，更严格的监管环境将有利于有完善合规体系的⽼牌机构，⽽⾮规范程度较低的
新兴平台。上⽉， Cboe 已获美国监管机构批准，成为⾸家获准上市与上市公司业绩指标挂钩的
期权合约的证券交易所。

预测市场⽅⾯，在特朗普政府的⽀持下当前享有强劲的联邦政策背书，若⺠主党胜选，同样可
能⾯临监管压⼒和法律挑战。
各⽅关注点：⺠主党如何排定施政优先级
多位分析师指出，⺠主党若上台，其施政重⼼的排序将在很⼤程度上决定数字⾦融⾏业的实际
⾛向。
Hyperion DeFi Inc. ⾸席执⾏官 Hyunsu Jung 表⽰，数字创新领域正处于快速发展阶段，但新多
数党可能将精⼒优先投⼊通胀回升和中东冲突等议题，同时允许 CFTC 和 SEC 继续推进规则制
定。
投资咨询机构 Paradigm ⾼级顾问 Justin Slaughter 亦持相近看法： " ⺠主党⾯临的抉择是 —— 集中
⼒量打好少数⼏场硬仗，还是全⾯出击？ "
StoneX 的 Palmer 则将视线延伸⾄更远处。他表⽰，随着 2026 年中期选举结束、 2028 年总统⼤
选竞争进⼊实质阶段，市场对不同候选⼈加密政策⽴场的审视与推演将明显升温， " 围绕加密空
间的争议和猜测将相当可观，两党皆然 " 。

3081  TT 聯亞

矽光模組 CW 雷射供應持續吃緊，聯亞獲美系 CSP 四年長約，並增加基板
供應源，且資本支出再次增加以實現 2028 年雷射產出量倍增，未來三年成
長無慮。研究員預估2026~2028年營收將續創新高，惟考量公司定價策略審
慎，毛利率難大幅攀升，加上擴產帶動營業費用增加，下修 2027年 EPS預
估至 50.4元，考量其產業地位及成長性，維持 78倍本益比評價，目標價由
4,100 元調降至 3,930元(78x 2027E EPS)，維持「強力買進」評等。     
投資評等 強力買進  重點摘要 
目標價位 3,930 元  一、 聯亞為全球少數具晶圓製程能力的 InP雷射純代工廠，
獲美系客戶 4年長約，再次擴大資本支出至 80億。 
聯亞成立於1997年，從既有InP磊晶晶圓代工延伸至雷射晶片
製造，提供涵蓋MOCVD磊晶、晶圓製程至雷射晶片量產的一
站式代工服務。目前聯亞約8成營收來自美系CSP AI資料中心，
主力產品以應用於矽光(SiPh)模組的CW雷射為主，隨產能持續
開出，也依客戶需求增加PD產品供應。

相較國內同業以磊晶晶圓代工為主，聯亞可生產雷射晶片；而
相比Lumentum、Coherent等國際雷射大廠採IDM模式，其InP雷
射產能主要供應自有產品，聯亞專注代工，雷射規格可由CSP
直接提出，或透過光模組廠導入，有利CSP掌握產品規格，並
降低對單一IDM供應商的依賴。

美系CSP為確保雷射穩定供貨，與聯亞簽訂4年長約，聯亞為穩
定InP基板供應，除原先與日廠住友電氣工業(Sumitomo)簽署5
年供貨合約外，又新增日系與美系基板商各一家。此外，為因
應美系客戶需求，公司於2026年8月再次擴大資本支出，從原有
近60億提高至80億元，預估2028年雷射產出量將增至2026年的
約6倍。

二、 3Q26 矽光產品出貨持續成長，營收 15.62億元，QoQ 
+27.9%、YoY +182.6%，EPS 6.12元，QoQ +32.5%、
YoY +466.7%。 
AI資料中心對矽光產品需求強勁，聯亞持續增加國內外後段代
工合作夥伴，且微影設備也陸續開出產能，增加 CW雷射供應
能力，帶動出貨成長。3Q26營收15.62億元，QoQ +27.9%、YoY

+182.6%；毛利率58.6%，較2Q26提升1.1個百分點；營業利益
7.71億元， QoQ +34.6% ；EPS 6.12 元， QoQ +32.5% 、YoY 
+466.7%。3Q26營業費用1.43億元，季增1,400萬元，主要反映
研發費用增加，以及獲利成長帶動員工分紅增加。整體營收表
現符合預期，EPS則優於預期。

三、 4Q26 矽光產品需求仍強勁，主要客戶訂單能見度超過 6
個月，研究員預估營收 18.12億元，QoQ +16.0%、YoY 
+182.0%，EPS 7.06元，QoQ +15.3%、YoY +241.0%。 
美系CSP客戶為避免雷射供應中斷影響光模組出貨，進而延誤
資料中心建置時程，提前下達長期訂單，目前主要客戶訂單能
見度已超過6個月。隨微影設備陸續開出產能，CW雷射供應能
力提升，預期出貨量將持續成長。研究員預估4Q26營收18.12億
元，QoQ +16.0%、YoY +182.0%；展望1Q27，隨更多新增產能
開出，產出數量增幅可望更為明顯。

毛利率方面，受惠營收規模擴大及產能利用率提升，有助固定
成本攤提，惟新設備陸續投入使用將增加折舊費用，且匯率變
化仍可能影響獲利表現。研究員預估4Q26毛利率59.5%，QoQ
增加0.9個百分點，YoY增加10.8個百分點；考量營業費用隨營
運規模增加，以及業外每季約1,500萬元可轉債利息費用，預估
EPS為7.06元，QoQ +15.3%、YoY +241.0%。

四、 受惠美系 CSP 長約訂單及持續擴產，聯亞 2026~2028 年
業績成長無慮。研究員預估2026年營收 54.99億元，YoY 
+149.7%，EPS 21.3元，YoY +364.7%；2027年營收 124
億元，YoY +125.5%，EPS 50.4元，YoY +137.5%。 
美系CSP為確保CW雷射供應穩定，與聯亞簽訂4年長期供貨合
約，聯亞亦持續擴大資本支出，並透過增加InP基板供應來源及
擴充產能因應客戶需求，預計2027、2028年產出數量分別年增
150%，2028年雷射產出規模約為2026年的6倍。

另外，聯亞已與客戶共同開發及驗證應用於3.2T、NPO及CPO
的高功率CW雷射產品，隨高速光互連朝高功率、多波長規格
升級，有助提升高階產品比重，研究員預期2026~2028年營收將
持續成長。

### Page 3

福邦投顧 股市個股早報 
        2026/10/29

G r a n d  F o r t u n e  S e c u r i t i e s                                                                                 3  
本報告係就內部參考用，純屬參考性質，並不具備任何法律效果，其正確性不做任何承諾或保證。 
研究員預估 2026年營收 54.99億元， YoY +149.7% ，毛利率
58.0%，EPS 21.3元，YoY +364.7%；2027年隨新增MOCVD及
微影設備產能陸續開出，以及1.6T光模組需求增加，預估營收
124億元，YoY +125.5%。毛利率方面，儘管CW雷射持續供不
應求，惟公司考量長期客戶合作關係，產品定價以維持合理利
潤為主，加上新產能投入將增加製造端折舊及人力成本，預期
毛利率不會持續大幅攀升，研究員預估2027年毛利率59.9%，考
量擴產帶動營業費用增加，下修EPS預估至50.43元。展望2028
年，隨新增產能持續釋出及高功率 CW雷射需求成長，預期營
收仍具成長空間。

五、 評等為「強力買進」的理由 
1. AI DC 傳輸需求帶動矽光快速成長，加上 InP 基板供
應有限，使 InP 雷射供給持續吃緊，聯亞為少數具備
InP 磊晶至雷射晶片製造能力的純代工廠，相較
Lumentum、Coherent 等 IDM 業者以自有產品為主，
公司可依 CSP或光模組廠需求客製雷射規格，具供應
鏈稀缺性。此外，聯亞加速產能擴張並取得 InP 基板
長約保障，產品則以 ASP 較佳、需求強勁的多波長
CW 雷射為主，產品組合轉佳，研究員預期聯亞未來
三年營運成長無慮。 
2. 國際光通訊 IDM 大廠於高速成長階段本益比區間為
50~70倍，考量 CW雷射供不應求而聯亞積極擴廠滿
足 CSP需求，且聯亞以磊晶與雷射晶片代工為主，商
業模式相對聚焦，於CW 雷射供需吃緊階段有望展現
較佳獲利彈性，故給予高於國際同業之本益比78 倍。
惟公司考量長期客戶關係，產品定價以維持合理利潤
為主，預期毛利率不會持續大幅攀升，且未來擴產將
增加人事、研發及設備折舊費用，匯率變化亦為獲利
影響因素。研究員考量擴產帶動營業費用增加，下修
2027 年 EPS預估，由原先 52.5 元降至 50.4 元，目標
價由 4,100 元調降至 3,930 元(78x 2027E EPS) ，維持
「強力買進」評等。

 3105  TT 穩懋

穩懋為全球三五族代工龍頭直接受惠低軌衛星爆發成長射頻代工需求 ， 加上光通
客戶 PD 專案放量貢獻，疊加權益法認列聯茂獲利大量挹注，2027 預估 EPS 為
25.11 元。依據 SOTP 估值法，將本業貢獻 17.38 元給予光通訊、低軌衛星行業
平均 40 倍本益比，以及聯茂貢獻 7.73 元給予 20 倍本益比，目標價 850 元，投
資建議為強力買進。         
投資評等 強力買進  重點摘要 
目標價位 850 元  一、穩懋為砷化鎵代工廠，產品重心逐漸自手機相關功率放大器
轉移向資料中心應用積極發展。 
穩懋半導體成立於 1999 年，為全球首家純 6 吋砷化鎵（GaAs）
晶圓代工廠，座落於林口華亞科技園區，專精 GaAs MMIC/RFIC
量產。公司提供 HBT、pHEMT、BiHEMT 及 GaN 等製程平台，
並延伸至設計佈局、晶圓測試與品質驗證等一站式服務。產品主
要應用於手機與物聯網功率放大器（PA）、Wi-Fi、基地台與通訊
基礎建設；近年亦將重心移向衛星、航太與AI 資料中心等高門檻
應用，其中以光收發模組相關產品最受市場期待。

4Q26 預估營收為 60.05 億元，QoQ+2.49%、YoY+25.25%。行動
通訊業務步入傳統淡季，且中低階機種需求受到成本上升壓力壓
抑；所幸iOS高階機型需求持續成長，有效抵銷中低階疲弱衝擊。
在此同時，雖逢第四季工廠例行性歲修，但低軌衛星受惠大客戶
換代逐步放量，產值貢獻大幅提升，帶動基礎設施應用維持強勁
成長，支撐單季營收微幅季增至 60.05 億元。 
獲利結構方面，預估毛利率為 37.26%，QoQ-0.14 個百分點、
YoY+5.46 個百分點。單季毛利率與 EPS 微幅季減，主要受到第
四季歲修略微影響產能稼動率；但受惠低軌衛星持續成長優化產
品組合，加上整體營收規模維持高檔具規模效應，支撐毛利率與
獲利維持高位，預估單季 EPS 為 4.55 元， QoQ-0.08%、
YoY+87.43%。

成本上升壓抑需求之衝擊，推升全年度營收規模擴張至 217.11 億

獲利結構方面，預估毛利率為 32.80%，YoY+8.61 個百分點。全
年度毛利率顯著改善，主要反映低軌衛星與光通訊應用領域毛利
率皆高於平均水準，隨著兩大高毛利專案放量出貨，帶動整體產

品組合帶動下，全年度獲利倍數成長，預估全年度 EPS 為 12.53
元，YoY+213.67%。 
五、2027年受惠低軌衛星換代與北美光通訊 PD專案大幅放量，
營收預估為 310.32億元，YoY+42.93%。獲利受惠產品組合優化
與聯茂權益法認列大量挹注，預估毛利率為 39.69%，預估 EPS
為 25.11元，YoY+100.38%。 
2027 年預估營收為 310.32 億元，YoY+42.93%。全年度營收大幅
成長突破 300 億元大關，主要受惠低軌衛星進一步放量，規格換
代加上 DTC 應用提升產值貢獻，帶動基礎設施應用領域強勁成
長；同時，北美光通訊大客戶 PD 專案正式迎來放量，帶來大量
營收挹注，多項動能齊發推升全年度營收規模躍升至 310.32 億
元。 
獲利結構方面，預估毛利率為 39.69%，YoY+6.89個百分點。全
年度毛利率維持高位，主要反映低軌衛星成長持續挹注產品組合
優化，儘管些微受到折舊上升影響，但整體高毛利產品比重擴大
帶動毛利率持續成長。此外，獲利部分受惠轉投資聯茂權益法認
列大量挹注，帶動全年度獲利動能續創新高，預估全年度 EPS
為 25.11 元，YoY+100.38%。 
六、評等為強力買進之理由 
(1) 穩懋為全球化合物半導體三五族晶圓代工龍頭廠商，受惠於
低軌衛星爆發成長所帶來之射頻元件代工強勁需求；同時，
北美光通訊大客戶 PD 專案將於明年迎來大規模放量，雙引
擎全面驅動整體營收與獲利高速成長。 
(2) 評價方面，依據SOTP評價法，將2027 年預估每股盈餘 25.11
元進行拆分：本業貢獻之 17.38 元給予光通訊與低軌衛星同
業 40 倍本益比評價，聯茂權益法認列貢獻之 7.73 元給予 20
倍本益比評價，目標價為 850 元，投資建議為強力買進。

## Document 791: 秋の中銀祭のポイント

Source PDF: all report pdf/秋の中銀祭のポイント.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 30 日(金)

1/4 
秋の中銀祭のポイント  
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・今週は10月27～28日にＦＯＭＣ、28～29日にＥＣＢ理事会、29～30日に日銀の金融政策決定
会合が予定されている。９月の３中銀一斉利上げから一転して10月は３中銀ともに据え置き
が予想されている。以下でそれぞれの概要を整理する。

・ＦｅｄはＦＦ金利（誘導目標上限値）を4.00％に据え置くだろう。ＦＦ金利先物から逆算し
た利上げ確率は10％程度と低い。いくら事前に情報を与えることを好まないウォーシュ議長
でも、サプライズ利上げで金融市場に無用の混乱を引き起こす選択はしないとみられる。中
間選挙前ということもあってサプライズ利上げを実施する意味に乏しい。もっとも、12月会
合の利上げ確率は90％と高水準にある。原油価格が高止まりするなか、財（エネルギー）を
中心に上振れリスクが強く残存しているため、政策当局として様子見を続けることは難し
い。

・一方で、インフレの基調判断で重視すべきサービス価格は安定している。まず家賃について
は、住宅ローン金利が高止まりするなか、今後も上昇率が鈍化する公算が大きい。住宅ロー
ン金利が７％を超すもとで、中古住宅販売件数は年率400万件程度まで落ち込み、これはリー
マンショック直後の最悪期に等しい。住宅建設業者の景況感を示すNAHB住宅市場指数は歴史
・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000 円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPY は先行き12 ヶ月 155 円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12 月に 1.5％とした後、27 年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FED はFF 金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/4 
的低水準に落ち込んでいる。住宅着工件数も水準を切り下げている。ちなみに、期間30年の
固定金利を７％で借り入れた場合、元利均等返済だと最初の５年間で元本は5.9％しか減ら
ず、10年間でも14.3％しか減らない（元金均等返済の場合は５年毎に当初元本の16.7％が減
少）。

・そして家賃以外のサービス価格に重大な影響を与える労働コストについては、雇用統計で示
される平均時給を見る限り、反転上昇は予想しにくい。ＮＦＩＢ中小企業調査によれば、３
ヶ月先の人件費計画を問う項目の数値は過去２年程度の下限付近で推移している。すなわち
現在のインフレは、財価格が関税と原油高によって押し上げられている一方、サービス価格
は落ち着いている、という整理になる。

### Page 3

3/4 
・こうした状況下、Ｆｅｄ中枢のジェファーソン副議長、ウォラー理事、ウィリアムズ・ＮＹ
連銀総裁は、９月ＦＯＭＣ以降、連続利上げを急がない姿勢を示している。このうちウィリ
アムズ総裁は、年内にあと１回の利上げが適切となる可能性を指摘する一方、現時点では
2027年に追加利上げが必要になるとは考えていないとの見解を示している。

・次にＥＣＢ理事会に目を向けると、こちらも政策金利の据え置きが広く予想されている。中
銀預金金利は2.5％となろう。ＯＩＳ金利から逆算した10月の利上げ確率は４％弱と低いもの
の、12月理事会では90％程度と極めて高い。ユーロ圏の９月ＣＰＩは前年比＋3.8％と８月か
ら0.6％pt加速、コアＣＰＩは同＋2.5％と過去２年程度のレンジ内に収まっているものの、
エネルギー価格高騰が賃金上昇を通じてサービス価格に波及することを予防する意味もあ
り、筆者も12月は利上げに踏み切ると予想している。労働者（組合）の交渉力が強いユーロ
圏では、インフレの二次的波及が起こりやすい。

・そうした特性を象徴するのが、2011年の利上げである。当時は欧州債務危機の最中で、金融
市場の緊張感は高まっていたが、「アラブの春」と言われる中東・北アフリカ情勢の緊迫化
を背景とする原油高が、ユーロ圏のインフレを加速させ得るとの判断からトリシェ総裁（当
時）は利上げを断行した。その後のドラギ体制ではデフレの脅威と闘ったこともあってＦｅ
ｄに近い考え方を採用し積極的な金融緩和を実施していたが、コロナ期のインフレを経て再
び物価に敏感な姿勢に回帰している。

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
4/4 
・そして日銀の金融政策決定会合も、政策金利の据え置きが広く予想されている。そのうえで
筆者は①高田委員と田村委員の投票行動、②浅田委員と佐藤委員の物価認識に注目してい
る。

・高田委員は、政策金利の据え置きに対して１月、３月、４月、７月の４回の会合で反対票を
投じており、今回も利上げを主張するとみられる。田村委員は４月の据え置きに際しては利
上げを主張して反対票を投じたものの、７月は賛成票を投じており、今回どう動くかは興味
深い。仮に田村委員も利上げを主張するならば、次回以降の利上げ観測を強める要因になろ
う。

・浅田委員と佐藤委員が、基調的な物価上昇率が２％に到達したとの認識に対して異論を唱え
る可能性がある。両委員は、日本経済の足腰の弱さを理由に９月会合で利上げに反対票を投
じた経緯があり、今回もハト派色を前面に押し出す可能性がある。仮にそうした主張をすれ
ば、円安を誘発し得る。一方で、基調的な物価上昇率の２％到達を認める場合は、９月下旬
頃から相次いでいる高市首相、片山財務相、木内経済財政相の「脱リフレ」に同調したとの
見方が広がるのではないか。そうなれば円高を促し得る。

## Document 799: 米国10月ISM製造業指数は予想を上振れ、仕入価格は高止まり ～AI・防衛等の需要が底上げ、インフレ懸念に伴う米金利上昇・ドル強含みへ～

Source PDF: all report pdf/米国10月ISM製造業指数は予想を上振れ、仕入価格は高止まり   ～AI・防衛等の需要が底上げ、インフレ懸念に伴う米金利上昇・ドル強含みへ～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok
